### [1] [Imaging Compressible and Incompressible Kinetic Energy of a 2d Bose-Einstein Condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31502)
*Elinor Kath,Jelte Duchêne,Hanyi Jang,Floriane Arrouas,Helmut Strobel,Markus K. Oberthaler*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 提出基于速度敏感双光子跃迁的方法，可在单次实现中重构玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体的二维速度场与局域原子密度，并在量子化涡旋上定量标定；应用于非平衡凝聚体时揭示流动动能的可压缩分量快速衰减、不可压缩分量长期主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有速度场测量通常需多次重复实现或依赖破坏性成像，难以在单次实现中同时获得速度场与密度分布，限制了对非平衡动力学（尤其可压缩/不可压缩流动分量演化）的定量研究。

Method: 采用对速度敏感的双光子跃迁（velocity-sensitive two-photon transitions）作为探测手段，通过跃迁谱的Doppler/速度选择性响应，在单次实验实现中同时重建二维速度场与局域原子密度；并以量子化涡旋作为已知解析解的基准体系进行定量标定（benchmark）。

Result: 方法在量子化涡旋上得到定量验证；应用于远离平衡的凝聚体时，测得流动动能的可压缩分量迅速衰减，而不可压缩分量持续存在，并在长时间动力学中占主导。

Conclusion: 该单次实现的二维速度场与密度联合测量方法可靠且可定量校验，能够分辨流动动能的不同分量，为研究量子流体非平衡湍流与弛豫动力学提供有力工具。

Abstract: We introduce a method based on velocity-sensitive two-photon transitions to reconstruct the two-dimensional velocity field as well as the local atomic density of a Bose-Einstein condensate in a single realization. We quantitatively benchmark the method on a quantized vortex. Applied to a condensate far from equilibrium, the method reveals the contrasting behaviors of the system's flow kinetic energy components, where the compressible component quickly decays while the incompressible one remains, dominating the long-time dynamics.

</details>


### [2] [Parametric neural-network wavefunctions across a bosonic phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33150)
*Daniil S. Antonenko,Liang Fu,Leonid I. Glazman*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 提出Boseformer——一个基于注意力的通用神经网络架构，用于表示玻色子波函数（含超流相与晶体相），可跨量子相变参数化生成波函数并作为基础模型使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络波函数通常针对单一哈密顿量参数单独训练，缺乏可跨相变、跨参数泛化的统一表示框架；本文希望构建一个"基础模型"，无需对每个参数值重新训练。

Method: 设计注意力机制神经网络Boseformer，将波函数表示为哈密顿量参数的函数（而非固定参数下的单一波函数），并用变分蒙特卡洛（VMC）优化训练；在二维玻色子系统、1/r^3 排斥相互作用上验证，覆盖超流—晶体量子相变。

Result: 训练后的单一模型可刻画整段相变过程及不同参数下的基态波函数，无需针对个别参数值重新优化，在超流相与晶体相均给出有效描述。

Conclusion: 参数化、注意力的神经网络波函数可作为量子多体系统的"基础模型"，实现对量子相变的统一描述，为跨参数泛化的变分表示提供可行路线。

Abstract: We propose Boseformer, a universal attention-based neural-network architecture for representing the wavefunctions of particles with bosonic quantum statistics, including superfluid and crystal phases. The model is trained to generate the wavefunction as a function of a Hamiltonian parameter across the quantum phase transition between these phases. Once trained with variational Monte Carlo optimization, it can serve as a foundation model describing the phase transition without the need to retrain it for individual parameter values. We demonstrate our method on a system of two-dimensional bosons with $1/r^3$ repulsive interaction.

</details>


### [3] [Detecting Topological Phases Through Dynamics of Chiral Density in Momentum-space Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31878)
*Rui Tian,Xiayao He,Tianhao Wu,Dongdong Pu,Shuai Li,Bo Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 提出利用手征算符动力学的手征密度演化来重构动量空间SSH链的局域态密度与态密度，从而无歧义地探测零能边缘态与体-边对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动量空间晶格是量子模拟（尤其拓扑物态）的通用平台，但需要可靠的谱学手段来诊断其拓扑性质。

Method: 设计基于手征算符动力学的谱学协议：证明手征密度的时间演化与局域态密度（LDOS）直接相关，并对其作傅里叶变换以重构LDOS与总态密度，对象为动量空间Su-Schrieffer-Heeger链。

Result: 重构出的LDOS与态密度可清晰给出零能边缘模式的信号，并验证体-边对应关系。

Conclusion: 该动力学方法为在合成量子物质中探索拓扑相提供了一种可操作的诊断工具。

Abstract: Momentum-space lattices offer a versatile platform for quantum simulation in particular emulating topological states of matter.In this work, we propose a spectroscopic protocol via utilizing the dynamics of the chiral operator to detect the topological properties of a momentum-space Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chain. We show that the time evolution of the chiral density is directly linked to the local density of state (LDOS). By performing a Fourier transformation of that, we reconstruct both the LDOS and density of states of the system, enabling the unambiguous signature of zero-energy edge modes and the bulk-boundary correspondence. This dynamical approach provides a diagnostic tool for exploring topological phases in synthetic quantum matter.

</details>


### [4] [Coherent meson-baryon conversion in a cold-atom non-Abelian lattice gauge simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31888)
*Tian-Yi Wang,Wei-Yong Zhang,Tie-Jin Zhong,Ming-Gen He,Zhao-Yu Zhou,Hui Sun,Zhen-Sheng Yuan,Jian-Wei Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 利用87Rb冷原子模拟量子模拟器，将两个超精细态与格点占据组态分别编码物质场与规范场，在自旋依赖光学超晶格中实现截断的一维SU(2)量子链接模型，并直接观测到介子向重子-反重子或类真空态的转化动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCD的实时动力学与有限密度性质因符号问题难以用传统蒙特卡洛方法研究；数字量子模拟受线路深度与噪声限制，模拟系统又难以工程化非阿贝尔规范-物质耦合，因此需要新的实验平台。

Method: 以87Rb冷原子为模拟量子模拟器：用两个超精细态与格点占据组态联合编码物质场和规范场；通过自旋依赖光学超晶格中的共振隧穿实现截断一维SU(2)量子链接模型的一个规范扇区，并独立调节质量与规范耦合；借助位点与自旋分辨量子气体显微术，通过缓慢质量扫描追踪初态介子向重子或类真空组态的转化。

Result: 在共振附近观测到介子与重子-反重子对之间的相干振荡，并给出转化效率随质量、规范耦合和净重子密度的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了非阿贝尔规范动力学的原子平台，为强相互作用物质中非平衡动力学的微观研究开辟了途径。

Abstract: Quantum chromodynamics (QCD), the non-Abelian gauge theory of quarks and gluons, provides a microscopic description of strongly interacting matter, including hadron structure and dynamics. Real-time dynamics and finite-density properties of these systems remain challenging for conventional Monte Carlo methods because of sign problems. Quantum simulation provides a complementary approach, although circuit depth and noise limit digital implementations and non-Abelian gauge-matter interactions remain difficult to engineer in analog systems. Here we realize a non-Abelian lattice gauge model with dynamical matter in an analog cold-atom simulator and directly observe its hadronic dynamics using site- and spin-resolved quantum gas microscopy. This realization is enabled by harnessing two hyperfine states and site-occupation configurations of 87Rb atoms to jointly encode both matter and gauge fields. We tailor resonant tunneling in a spin-dependent optical superlattice to implement a gauge sector of a truncated one-dimensional SU(2) quantum link model with independently tunable mass and gauge coupling. Leveraging this control, we track the conversion of an initially mesonic state into baryonic or vacuum-like configurations through slow mass ramps. Near resonance, we observe coherent oscillations between mesons and baryon-antibaryon pairs and characterize the conversion efficiency as a function of mass, gauge coupling and net baryon density. Our realization establishes an atomic platform for non-Abelian gauge dynamics, opening a route to microscopic studies of nonequilibrium dynamics in strongly interacting matter.

</details>


### [5] [Relaxation dynamics of the impurity momentum in a one-dimensional Fermi gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32280)
*Felipe Taha Sant'Ana*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文针对一维中与无自旋自由费米子气体通过排斥接触作用耦合的单个移动杂质，利用相互作用本征态与自由费米子态之间的行列式重叠，推导出杂质动量算符及其平方在有限系统任意Bethe-ansatz本征态之间的闭合行列式矩阵元公式，并据此解析研究注入动力学与热力学极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂质在量子多体环境中的动量弛豫是开放量子系统与可积模型交叉的核心问题，但以往缺乏对有限系统全部本征态之间杂质动量算符矩阵元的显式、可计算表达式，因而难以把谱表示转化为可控的有限求和并严格讨论热力学极限。

Method: 以一维排斥接触相互作用耦合的移动杂质—无自旋自由费米子模型为对象，借助相互作用本征态与自由费米子态的行列式重叠，推导杂质动量算符及其平方在任意Bethe-ansatz本征态间矩阵元的闭合行列式公式；在无限耦合极限证明选择定则；将平均杂质动量的谱表示化为对本征态的显式有限求和，数值评估注入后动力学并映射稳态动量随注入动量与耦合强度的依赖；最后把热力学极限及其渐近行为写成Fredholm行列式的积分。

Result: 得到杂质动量算符及其平方的闭合行列式矩阵元；证明无限耦合下的选择定则：两算符在共享同一rapidity的不同本征态之间矩阵元为零，且每个本征态携带总动量的按粒子均分份额；发现无限耦合时注入杂质的平均动量在热力学极限下完全弛豫，而有限耦合下弛豫不完全；解析分辨出量子颤振（quantum flutter）振荡与趋向对角系综值的过程，并给出稳态动量随注入动量与耦合强度的相图。

Conclusion: 闭合行列式矩阵元为杂质动量动力学提供了精确且可数的谱表示框架；弛豫是否完全由耦合强度决定，无限耦合为完全弛豫的临界情形；热力学极限可由Fredholm行列式积分统一描述，为可积杂质模型的非平衡动力学与广义流体力学描述提供严格基础。

Abstract: We consider a single mobile impurity coupled by a repulsive contact interaction to a gas of free spinless fermions of equal mass in one dimension. Using the determinant overlaps between the interacting eigenstates and free-fermion states, we derive closed determinant formulas for the matrix elements of the impurity momentum operator and of its square between arbitrary Bethe-ansatz eigenstates of a finite system. At infinite coupling we prove a selection rule: both operators have vanishing matrix elements between distinct eigenstates that share the same rapidity, and every eigenstate carries the per-particle share of the total momentum. For an impurity injected into the Fermi sea we have the following: at infinite coupling the average impurity momentum relaxes completely in the thermodynamic limit, while at finite coupling relaxation remains incomplete. The closed-form matrix elements turn the spectral representation of the average impurity momentum into explicit finite sums over eigenstates. We evaluate the post-injection dynamics, resolve the quantum-flutter oscillations and the approach towards the diagonal-ensemble value, and map the stationary momentum as a function of the injection momentum and the coupling. Finally, we analyse the thermodynamic limit and its asymptotics as an integral over Fredholm determinants.

</details>


### [6] [Probing the Rung-Mott Insulator in Bosonic Two-Leg Ladders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32680)
*Christopher Parsonage,Lennart Koehn,Lorenzo Carfora,Ben Watson,Timon Hilker,Elmar Haller,Peter Kirton,Arthur La Rooij,Stefan Kuhr*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 利用超冷玻色原子在近半填充的双腿梯子中实现梯级Mott绝缘相（RMI），通过位点分辨测量识别其梯级占据，并用晶格调制谱学探测激发谱，结合含驱动动力学与多激发态耦合的数值模拟解释谱线，同时以密度—化学势的可压缩性分析确认其不可压缩性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mott绝缘体通常出现在整数密度，但在特定晶格几何或非局域相互作用下可在分数密度稳定存在；本文旨在实验上识别并刻画近半填充双腿梯子体系中的分数密度Mott相（rung-Mott绝缘相）及其激发动力学。

Method: 在可编程晶格中的超冷玻色原子双足梯子（近半填充）上，采用量子气体显微镜的位点分辨测量确定梯级占据以识别RMI相；用晶格调制谱学测量激发谱；用包含驱动动力学、多个激发态耦合和能量偏置的数值模拟对谱进行解释；通过位点分辨的密度与化学势分析检验不可压缩性。

Result: 在位点分辨测量中观测到特征性的梯级占据，确认了RMI相；晶格调制谱学给出激发谱，其结构与含驱动动力学的数值模拟定量吻合；密度—化学势分析显示出不可压缩行为，与Mott绝缘体特征一致。

Conclusion: 实验直接揭示了强关联梯子体系的激发动力学，验证了分数密度梯级Mott绝缘相的存在与性质，并为在可编程晶格几何的低维量子系统中研究输运及进一步动力学性质奠定基础。

Abstract: Mott insulators typically appear at integer densities, but certain lattice geometries or non-local interactions can stabilise Mott insulators at fractional densities. In this work, we study strongly interacting two-leg ladders using ultracold bosonic atoms around half-filling, and identify the rung-Mott insulating (RMI) phase through its characteristic rung occupation in site-resolved measurements. We probe its excitation spectrum via lattice modulation spectroscopy, and interpret the observed spectra using numerical simulations that include the driven dynamics, coupling to multiple excited states, and energy offsets. As with other Mott insulators, the RMI phase is characterized through its incompressibility, which we probe via a site-resolved analysis of densities and chemical potentials. Our results provide direct insight into the excitation dynamics of a strongly correlated ladder system, and pave the way for studying transport and further dynamical properties in low-dimensional quantum systems with programmable lattice geometries.

</details>


### [7] [Exact solution of the Bose-Fermi mixture along the unnesting line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32804)
*Soham Chandak,Aleksandra Petković,Zoran Ristivojevic*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文在嵌套Bethe ansatz可"解嵌套"的特殊参数线上，将一维接触排斥Bose-Fermi混合体模型约化为Lieb-Liniger模型与吸引型Yang-Gaudin模型的已知解，并给出基态能量、激发速度与Drude权重的解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维Bose-Fermi混合体的嵌套Bethe ansatz解形式复杂，难以直接提取物理量；作者希望找到可解耦的特殊参数线，借助两个已知可积模型的成熟结果获得解析预言。

Method: 采用嵌套Bethe ansatz；选取Bethe ansatz方程可解嵌套（unnested）的特殊参数线，将解表示为Lieb-Liniger模型（玻色子）与平衡情形下吸引自旋-1/2费米子Yang-Gaudin模型解的叠加/组合；在弱、强相互作用极限下做解析展开。

Result: 得到基态能量、激发速度（声速等）以及Drude权重的显式解析表达式，覆盖弱相互作用与强相互作用两个极限；弱相互作用下该特殊线对应自由费米子与弱相互作用玻色子系统速度相等的参数点。

Conclusion: 该特殊线提供了一个可解析处理的窗口，将混合体物理映射到两个已知可积模型，从而定量刻画其基态与输运（Drude权重）性质。

Abstract: A one-dimensional model of a Bose-Fermi mixture with contact repulsions is exactly solvable via nested Bethe ansatz. That formal solution is here simplified along a special line where the Bethe-ansatz equations can be unnested. The model solution is expressed in terms of the known solutions for the Lieb-Liniger model and the Yang-Gaudin model of attractive spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ fermions in the balanced case. We find explicit analytical results for the ground-state energy, the velocities of excitations, and the Drude weights at weak and strong interactions. At weak interactions, the special line corresponds to the parameter values where the isolated systems of free fermions and weakly interacting bosons have equal velocities.

</details>

### [8] [Tunable first-order transitions and magnetic hysteresis in Bernal pentalayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31376)
*Wentian Zheng,Jiawei Hu,Yuxuan Yao,Fang Xie,Zihan Wang,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Qimiao Si,Yonglong Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在Bernal堆叠五层石墨烯中，利用局域压缩率测量揭示了完全对称性破缺的朗道能级谱与一系列子带朗道能级交叉处的相变，这些相变表现为磁场下具有迟滞的霍尔响应，其迟滞幅度与符号可由位移场调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维电子体系中强库仑相互作用可自发解除自旋、谷等味简并，产生具有迟滞开关特性的第一类相变，对存储器件有应用价值。多带体系中的子带自由度可视为一种"味"，既参与此类相变，又可通过位移场调节子带间能量差来实现对迟滞行为的控制，本文旨在验证这一原理。

Method: 选用Bernal堆叠五层石墨烯（低能谱包含一个类单层狄拉克子带及轻、重两个类双层子带）作为平台；采用局域压缩率测量（扫描探针类热力学测量）解析朗道能级谱与子带朗道能级交叉处的相变序列；并通过磁场下的霍尔输运测量考察迟滞响应，结合位移场进行调控。

Result: 压缩率测量分辨出完全对称性破缺的朗道能级谱，以及在不同子带朗道能级交叉时发生的一系列相变，强相互作用在这些交叉点重新分配各味间的占据。这些相变在磁场下表现出迟滞的霍尔响应，证实其第一类相变性质；迟滞的幅度与符号随位移场变化，与压缩率测量所确定的子带朗道能级次序预期一致。

Conclusion: 本工作确立了多带体系中的子带自由度作为工程化迟滞开关的可利用资源，并表明局域热力学测量能够将微观机理与量子材料的宏观响应联系起来。

Abstract: In two-dimensional electron systems, strong Coulomb interactions can spontaneously lift flavor degeneracies such as spin and valley, producing first-order transitions with hysteretic switching of interest for memory devices. The subband degree of freedom in multiband systems may be viewed as a flavor that not only participates in such transitions but also enables control of the hysteretic behavior, as a displacement field can shift the energy difference between subbands. Here, we demonstrate this principle in Bernal-stacked pentalayer graphene, whose low-energy spectrum comprises a monolayer-like Dirac sub-band together with light and heavy bilayer-like sub-bands. Local compressibility measurements resolve a fully symmetry-broken Landau-level spectrum and a sequence of phase transitions that occur when Landau levels of different subbands cross, at which strong interactions redistribute the occupation among flavors. We show that these transitions exhibit hysteretic Hall responses in magnetic field, confirming their first-order character, and that the magnitude and sign of the hysteresis are tuned by the displacement field, as expected from the subband Landau-level ordering determined in compressibility measurements. Our work establishes the subband degree of freedom in multiband systems as a resource for engineering hysteretic switching, and highlights how local thermodynamic measurements can link microscopic insights to macroscopic responses in quantum materials.

</details>


### [9] [Moiré-enabled polariton control in large-angle twisted bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31423)
*Bogdan Borodin,Xuanpu Zhang,Christopher Yang,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Cyprian Lewandowski,Petr Stepanov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用双曲等离激元-声子极化激元（HP3）探测双层石墨烯从AB堆叠到5.5°扭转的动力学电子响应，发现一个由扭转角选择的窄密度窗口，其中极化激元波长强烈栅压可调，总波长变化近90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转双层石墨烯通常只在魔角附近的强关联平带和大扭转角近退耦狄拉克锥两个极限区制下被研究，中间扭转区制（如4°–5.5°）的集体电动力学行为尚不清楚，且缺乏对远莫尔带如何重塑动力学电导/德鲁德响应的直接实验探测手段。

Abstract: Twisted bilayer graphene (TBG) is commonly viewed through two limiting regimes: strongly correlated flat bands near the magic angle and nearly decoupled Dirac cones at large twist angles. (a) In the first approach, we construct the fractal Hamiltonian by removing all parent-lattice sites that do not belong to such a network (method of site elimination). (b) In the second one, we integrate or project out the same set of sites to obtain an effective Hamiltonian for the fractal lattice (method of projection or renormalization). (c) In the third approach, we retain the parent-crystal lattice intact but spatially pattern the on-site mass parameter, which controls the topology of the parent insulating system, such that it assumes two topologically distinct values on the fractal network and its complement (patterned fractal lattices). Result: 如定性预期，随着线长增加和衬底温度升高，电子-声子散射作用增强，散粒噪声被抑制；但在长导线的高偏压、高温极限，低于几开尔文时定量有效的简单电子-声子能量损失模型低估了过量噪声，且该偏差在SiO2/Si上的导线中比在裸Si上更严重。

Abstract: As a mesoscopic diffusive wire at low temperatures is driven out of equilibrium by a current, excess noise is observed compared to the equilibrium Johnson-Nyquist fluctuations. We measure noise as a function of bias current in diffusive Au nanowires on SiO2/Si with lengths ranging from sub-μm to 30 μm and temperatures from 3 K to 50 K, to map out the interplay between charge shot noise and the crossover to electron self-heating and phonon-dominated noise suppression. As expected qualitatively, shot noise is suppressed with increasing wire length and substrate temperature, as electron-phonon scattering is of increasing importance. In long wires, the simple model of electron-phonon energy loss known to be quantitatively effective below a few Kelvin underpredicts the excess noise in the high bias, high temperature limit. Abstract: Spin-logic devices promise non-volatile logic-in-memory computing with ultra-low power consumption, offering a route to circumvent the von Neumann bottleneck. Magnetoelectric and ferroelectric spin-orbit (MESO/FESO) devices, however, require spin-charge interconversion materials that combine a large output signal with properties that survive nanoscale integration. Conclusion: 该工作证实了莫尔超晶格中增强的层间电子-声子耦合，为理解与调控超导等新兴量子现象以及设计新型光电器件提供了途径。

Abstract: Electron-phonon coupling (EPC) plays a vital role in the electrical and optical properties of materials, ranging from superconductivity to charge dynamics.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平带系统中相干动力学可导致完全局域化（Aharonov-Bohm囚禁），限制输运调控；作者希望探索耗散环境是否能成为打破这种局域化、实现输运控制的手段。

Conclusion: 将平带系统与耗散环境耦合，是控制输运的一条可行途径，可在纯相干动力学完全局域化的参数区实现有效输运。

Abstract: We study quantum transport in a diamond chain coupled to Markovian reservoirs at its boundaries. We show that in the all-bands-flat regime at $Φ=π$, dephasing destroys Aharonov-Bohm caging, leading to environment-assisted quantum transport. Our results demonstrate that coupling a flat-band system to a dissipative environment provides a viable route for controlling transport in regimes where coherent dynamics alone would result in complete localization.

Abstract: Spin noise spectroscopy in back- and forward-scattering geometries is used to study the spin dynamics of charge carriers in a MAPbI$_3$ (MA = methylammonium) single crystal. The spectra reveal electrons and holes with different degrees of localization. At low temperatures ($T \approx 4$~K), hole spin relaxation is dominated by the hyperfine interaction with nuclear spins. The broadening of the spectra with increasing temperature can be attributed to thermally activated hopping of localized holes. From the thermal activation energies, we estimate hole localization lengths that are consistent with both the hyperfine-induced dephasing rates and the zero-field frequency shifts measured at low temperature. By contrast, the electron spin dynamics cannot be explained by the hyperfine interaction alone: even at the lowest excitation powers, it is affected by the probe light, and we observe a slowing down of spin relaxation with increasing power. Comparison of the back- and forward-scattered spin noise signals indicates that the spatial distribution of carriers is inhomogeneous: electrons are located preferentially near the crystal surface, weakly localized holes (characterized by slower spin relaxation) are found mostly away from the surface, and strongly localized holes are somewhere in between.

In spectrally isolated regimes, this redistribution switches the lower band between Chern numbers $\mathcal{C}=N$ and $\mathcal{C}=-1$. A projected surface theory shows how interfacial tunneling is dressed by virtual propagation through the rhombohedral stack, explaining the field-selected Chern hierarchy and the recurring threefold structure of the real-space charge textures. These results establish layer number and displacement field as complementary nanoscale controls of band flatness, spatial polarization, and topology in graphene moiré heterostructures.

Abstract: Competing interactions can generate frustration which suppresses macroscopic-scale phase separation, superseding it with separation patterns on the microscopic scale. This phenomenon, referred to as microphase separation, produces thermodynamically stable mesoscopic-scale periodic patterns in systems of different physical nature. The studies of this effect follow two paradigms. First, in block copolymers, the macroscopic-scale separation of chemically distinct components is prevented by the covalent bonds between them. In the other, the microscopic-scale separation is produced in simple single-component liquids by the competition between a short-range attraction and a long-range repulsion (SALR) in the pair potential, the separation occurring between a dense phase and a gas. The interest in the simple liquid models with SALR interaction was inspired by the apparent universality of the modulated structures generated by competing interactions in different systems. Simple particle models are tractable by theory and thereby make it possible to discern the microscopic mechanisms of pattern formation; particle-based simulations give access to systems beyond the reach of experiments. In this Perspective we review the recent advances in the molecular-dynamics simulation of microphase separation in single-component simple liquids using SALR potentials. We show that these simulations reproduce the mesophases typical of block copolymers and liquid crystals, which demonstrates that the complexity of the constituent particles of real liquids is not a necessary condition for their formation, and that they produce novel periodic morphologies of both conceptual interest and technological relevance; we discuss how simulated mesophases can serve as templates for metamaterials whose properties are governed by topology.

</details>

Abstract: Phonon thermal and angular-momentum (AM) Hall effects are known in non-ferroelectric insulators, yet whether terahertz soft modes of displacive ferroelectrics, i.e., ``ferrons", share them remains open. We report axial ferrons that hold polarization-momentum locking in uniaxial ferroelectrics and show that both effects exist: a longitudinal temperature gradient drives a transverse AM flow without any field, whereas an out-of-plane magnetic field switches on the thermal Hall response. Two competitive channels emerge for the AM Hall effect: self-radiated magnetic fields hybridize two branches into circular modes whose momentum-locked Berry curvature deflects opposite magnetic moments to opposite edges, while temperature-gradient-induced mode quantum coherence adds a magnetic-moment dipole that leads to an opposite transverse AM current. An out-of-plane magnetic field breaks the exact polarization-momentum locking and yields a polarization Hall current. Numerical calculations show these effects are significant in both LiNbO$_3$ and van der Waals ferroelectrics. Edge magnetization or polarization accumulation can switch adjacent magnets or ferroelectrics, enabling low-power magnetoelectric functionality by thermal current in the future.

Abstract: Chiral phonons, lattice vibrations carrying angular momentum, offer a new handle for controlling phonon-mediated phenomena in condensed matter, yet their on-demand generation and deterministic control remain challenging. Here we show, using helicity-resolved Raman spectroscopy, that asymmetrically stacked graphene moiré superlattices provide a reconfigurable platform for chiral phonons. We observe a finite degree of circular polarization for both chiral interlayer shear phonons and intralayer optical phonons. A chiral-phonon Raman-tensor model attributes this response to interfacial $C_{2}$-symmetry breaking together with Raman resonance within the van Hove singularity region. Reversing either the twist sense or the stacking order inverts the degree of circular polarization through the spatial transformations linking the corresponding stacking geometries. By tuning the excitation energy and twist angle, we achieve nearly full-range control of the degree of circular polarization of interlayer shear phonons, from $-1$ to $1$, while gate-induced interlayer potential redistribution enables substantial and reversible in situ modulation. These results establish asymmetrically stacked graphene moiré superlattices as a versatile platform for generating and controlling chiral phonons, with implications for optoelectronic and quantum technologies.

Similarly, the two former molecular junctions have quite similar trans-to-cis isomerization kinetics, while the latter has a lower switching rate. The energy level of the highest occupied molecular orbital, which controls the electron transfer in the molecular junction, is weakly influenced by the nature of the spacer, nor by the conformation of the azobenzene moieties. The large increase in the conductance of the molecular junction with azobenzene in the cis-state is explained by an increase in the electronic coupling energy between the molecule and the top electrode. Theoretical studies by Density Functional Theory and Non Equilibrium Green's Functions are qualitatively consistent with the experiments. We find a consistent structure-property trend, showing that the higher molecule/electrode coupling energy for the cis-state is explained by the top benzene ring of the azobenzene lying flat with respect to the top electrode for the thiophene, phenylthiophene and bithiophene spacers. On the contrary, we observe that a spacer with a carbon-carbon triple bond (acetylene thiophene) induces a large irreproducibility and unpredictability of the results.

Abstract: We study the scalar Schrödinger equation in a helicoidal metric when the axial direction is closed by Dirichlet walls built into the operator domain. With hard walls, the axial derivative cannot be replaced by a traveling-wave factor, and in a rotationally symmetric spinless cavity, time reversal makes the levels with azimuthal numbers $m$ and $-m$ exactly degenerate. We write the Hamiltonian as a Hermitian coupled-subband matrix and give an exact gauge-adapted variational representation. For a finite radius, the leading twist correction is quadratic, and changes sign at a critical aspect ratio; for an unbounded radial domain, it is linear in $|Ω_g|$. Retuning the aspect ratio through an isolated radial--axial resonance within one $m$ sector opens an avoided crossing with gap $Δ_{\mathrm{gap}}/E_R=1.648775\ldots|Ω_gR^2/L|^3$ to leading order. A transverse dipole transition forbidden without twist acquires an oscillator strength quadratic in the twist, with leading coefficient $0.0559408010$ for $R/L=2$ from perturbation theory. Dipole sum rules and the convergence of the currents serve as checks on the numerics. We also give the inverse-mass tensor required for a scalar realization and re-examine each statement and figure of the earlier article [Opt. Quantum Electron. \textbf{58}, 113 (2026)] within the corrected model.

Abstract: The low-field Hall resistivity of non-magnetic metals has an intrinsic quantum contribution that derives from the field-corrected Berry curvature and intrinsic magnetic moment of the Bloch states near the Fermi surface. However, its effect is usually overshadowed by the classical Lorentz-force contribution. Here we show that for the longitudinal Hall effect, which occurs in ferroaxial crystals when the applied magnetic field lies in the Hall plane, the Lorentz force contribution vanishes for the eight ferroaxial groups with a 3,4 or 6-fold rotation axis. This provides an opportunity to measure the quantum correction in isolation, but so far all measured instances of the effect fall in the remaining five ferroaxial groups of lower symmetry, where the Lorentz force dominates the signal. We compile a list of candidate ferroaxial metals with such high-order rotation axis and compute the Hall effect from first principles for representative candidates, identifying Ni$_3$P with $σ^{\rm H}_{xzx} = 28.4$ S/m/T as a promising material to unambiguously measure the intrinsic longitudinal Hall effect.

Abstract: Localization in continuum spectra known as the non-Hermitian skin effect is widely believed to be a consequence of nontrivial topology of bulk bands. However, the robustness of the non-Hermitian skin effect against nonlinear effects is still a nontrivial problem. We here reveal that single-bond nonlinearity on a boundary can recover extended modes in a non-Hermitian tight-binding model. We propose and analyze a one-dimensional minimal model, where we introduce a nonlinear phase shift of a hopping term only at boundary sites. We also propose a possible photonic setup to dynamically observe the nonlinearity-induced extended modes as a steady state. Our results thus challenge the conventional notion of the non-Hermitian skin effect by revealing its dependence on nonlinear boundary conditions.

Abstract: Spin space groups (SSGs) describe magnetic crystals in the limit of negligible spin-orbit coupling, where spatial and spin rotations decouple. Although algebraic enumerations of SSGs exist, their abstract mathematical approaches obscure the physical meaning of the resulting classifications, leaving the direct correspondence between SSGs and real-space magnetic orders unestablished. Here, we present a physically motivated framework for enumerating and classifying SSGs based on their translation holonomy---the image of the spatial lattice translation group under the spin homomorphism. The three classes of abelian images in $SO(3)$ divide SSGs into three basic translation types. Translation holonomy constrains the spin operations associated with point-group elements, yielding a complete enumeration of SSGs organized by a physical hierarchy. Within this framework, translation holonomy classifies SSGs and magnetic orders into 17 universal spin-lattice types, each with unambiguous Bragg-peak geometries and polarization signatures in neutron diffraction. Beyond organizing known magnetic orders, this framework directly predicts four universal physical phenomena: (i) exact magnetization compensation enforced by certain spin-lattice types; (ii) an exact group-theoretic criterion for the emergence of noncommuting Brillouin zones; (iii) a new type of 3Q magnetic order with vanishing octupoles; and (iv) the realization of strictly collinear momentum-space spin textures protected by reflection-staggered coplanar spin lattices. We also construct a spin space group database organized according to this physical hierarchy.

</details>

### [25] [Absence of $σ$-criticality in Short-Range Spin Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31584)
*C. M. Newman,D. L. Stein*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 证明Edwards-Anderson伊辛自旋玻璃中两类σ-临界性均不出现，从而排除σ-临界性，并提出基态对多重性的严格必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: σ-临界性是基态对多重性的充分条件；前作已排除第一类σ-临界性，需判定第二类是否可能存在，以彻底厘清该充分条件是否适用于EA模型。

Method: 以单个耦合（键）在特定基态中的"flexibility"刻画基态稳定性；定义第二类σ-临界性为某键位于正密度其他键的临界液滴边界中；通过界面与临界液滴的组合论证在任意维数下排除该情形。

Conclusion: 虽未解决基态对多重性问题，但去除了一个充分条件；并据此提出基态对多重性的严格必要条件。

Abstract: The stability of ground states in the Edwards-Anderson Ising spin glass in dimensions two and higher against perturbations of a single coupling can be quantified through the ``flexibility'' of that coupling in a particular ground state. The property of $σ$-criticality is one in which varying a single coupling can alter the flexibilities of a (strictly) positive density of couplings in an infinite system, and its presence provides a sufficient condition for the presence of multiple ground state pairs. In a previous paper it was shown for the EA spin glass that $σ$-criticality of the first kind, in which the critical droplet of a bond has a positive-density interface with the ground state, was ruled out in any dimension. The remaining possibility, called $σ$-criticality of the second kind, is one in which a bond lies in the critical droplet boundaries of a positive density of other bonds. We show here that this kind of $σ$-criticality is also absent, ruling out altogether the presence of $σ$-criticality in the EA spin glass. While this doesn't resolve the question of multiplicity of ground state pairs, it removes a sufficient condition for its presence. We then propose a rigorous necessary condition for ground state pair multiplicity.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文将无穷维极限下 Anderson 局域化的近期结果纳入"共形性丧失"（conformality lost）框架，即两个 RG 不动点碰撞并变为复数；由此自然地引出 BKT 型两参数标度流，并给出 ε=1/d 展开的现象学 RG 方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无穷维（d→∞）Anderson 局域化已被数值与解析研究较多，但缺乏统一的 RG 语言刻画；同时 PNAS 工作中"所有可观测量由 BKT 型两参数标度流控制"的猜想需要一个理论框架支撑。

Method: 把 d→∞ 的局域化结果映射到共形性丧失情景（两个不动点在 RG 流中碰撞并变为复数配对），据此引入类似 BKT 的两参数标度流；并以 ε=1/d≪1 为小量对 RG 方程作 ε 展开，构造现象学 RG 方程。

Conclusion: 共形性丧失为无穷维 Anderson 局域化提供了自洽的 RG 描述，并给出适用于 d≳4 的 ε=1/d 展开现象学方程，支持 BKT 型两参数标度控制的猜想。

Abstract: We show that the recent works on Anderson localization when the number of spatial dimensions $d\to\infty$ can be framed into the scenario dubbed it conformality lost [Kaplan et al., Phys. Rev. D 80, 125005 (2009)], which occurs when two fixed points of the RG collide and become complex. This framing makes it natural for a two-parameter scaling flow à la Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless (BKT) to control all the observables of the theory as conjectured in [Vanoni et al., PNAS 121, e2401955121 (2024)]. We also use this as a starting point for an $ε$ expansion of the RG equations in $ε=1/d\ll 1$, and find phenomenological RG equations which fit the numerical results in dimensions $d\gtrsim 4$.

</details>

Method: 非局域正则变换（对称性重局域化）；1D半满链上保持哈密顿量的变换交换在位矢量荷与非局域谱轴向荷；bosonization识别动量绕转T-对偶与不可逆拓扑缺陷；非微扰norm-resolvent界证明有限化学势下涌现动力学规范局域性；2D方格子π-flux Majorana模型与Kitaev蜂窝模型对比，构造Schwartz局域重局域化与两味U(2)四元数不变量；3D推导精确混合矢量-轴向通量响应，构造指数局域矢量U(1)荷的四元数拓扑模型及SU(2) Weyl双重态的no-go定理。

Conclusion: 空间非局域对称性与规范动力学非局域性可解耦；对称性重局域化是统一处理1D–3D紧致对称性、反常与拓扑的工具，但其可行性依赖格点结构、拓扑与反常，并存在明确no-go限制（如紧致SU(2) Weyl双重态不存在保持Weyl内容与哈密顿量局域性的平移不变高斯在位重局域化）。

Abstract: Spatially nonlocal symmetries need not imply nonlocal gauge dynamics. Symmetry relocalization yields exact or emergent gauge locality in 1D, 2D, and 3D, subject to lattice structure, topology, and anomalies.

Bosonization identifies momentum-winding T-duality, with a parity-projected operator flowing to a noninvertible topological defect. A nonperturbative norm-resolvent bound proves emergent dynamical gauge locality at finite chemical potential, while a doubled Chern-insulator parent reveals the duality's bulk origin and superconducting extensions reveal hidden compact symmetries.

Relocalization retains Schwartz locality for asymptotically local gauging, while AIII/AII parents clarify bulk charge origin and parity-anomaly inflow. Two flavors yield compact $U(2)$, a quaternionic invariant, and asymptotically local non-Abelian gauging.
  In 3D, an exact mixed vector-axial flux response is derived, while a distinct model supports an exponentially local vector $U(1)$ charge with strong quaternionic topology. A nonlocal map makes the charge and Kramers symmetry onsite while preserving exponential Hamiltonian locality. For a compact $SU(2)$ Weyl doublet, a no-go theorem forbids translation-preserving Gaussian onsite relocalization retaining Weyl content and Hamiltonian locality.

</details>

Conclusion: 高次谐波发射能量可作为量子多体动力学的灵敏探针，为利用强场光谱学探索与调控强关联电子物相开辟了途径。

Abstract: High harmonic generation (HHG) in solids typically produces a rigid frequency comb locked to exact integer multiples of the drive photon energy. In strongly correlated materials, however, the light field can dynamically transform the electronic state on the timescale of harmonic generation itself, potentially violating this rule. Here, we report giant blueshifted, non-integer HHG in a cuprate Mott insulator Sr$_2$CuO$_3$ produced by mid-infrared excitations. Concurrent transient reflectivity measurements and time-dependent exact-diagonalization simulations of a driven Hubbard model support a many-body temporal gating mechanism that suppresses light emission on the trailing edge of the pulse, thereby blueshifting radiated harmonics in the frequency domain. Our findings establish HHG emission energy as a sensitive probe of quantum many-body dynamics and pave the way for exploration and manipulation of correlated electronic phases of matter with strong-field spectroscopy.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$中四极序已被大量实验与理论研究，但其微观起源与各相互作用的相对能量尺度尚未澄清；尤其$z^2$四极的铁性有序难以解释，超交换、经典四极-四极相互作用、振动耦合以及向高能能级的虚激发各自贡献缺乏定量确定。

Abstract: Despite extensive experimental and theoretical studies of quadrupolar order in Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$, the microscopic origin and relative energy scales of the interactions driving it remain unresolved. In particular, the observed ferroic ordering of the $z^2$ quadrupole has remained difficult to explain, as the relative contributions of superexchange, classical quadrupole-quadrupole interactions, vibronic couplings, and virtual excitations to higher-energy levels have not been established quantitatively. Here, we determine all these interactions from first principles and solve the resulting microscopic Hamiltonian. The calculated ground state reproduces the experimentally observed quadrupolar order parameters and the associated structural distortions with quantitative accuracy. Disentangling the individual contributions, we find that ferroic $z^2$ order originates from a linear-quartic coupling, generated by virtual excitations to the $J_{\mathrm{eff}}$= 1/2 doublet and driven by vibronic interactions. Our results establish the hierarchy of microscopic interactions underlying quadrupolar order in Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$ and provide a unified $ab \ initio$ description of its coupled electronic and lattice degrees of freedom.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角晶格自旋-1/2 XXZ 反铁磁体是阻挫量子磁性的范例体系，但其在一般各向异性下的零温基态相图以及热力学（有限温）相结构仍未被完整理解，特别是非经典超固态的稳定区间、它与半经典 V 相及磁化平台相的连接关系，以及有限温是否存在内禀有序相，均缺乏系统判定。

Conclusion: 三角晶格 XXZ 模型在高场区存在比既有认识更丰富的相结构：Ψ 超固态具有跨各向异性的普适稳定性，且强易轴区存在由畴壁物理驱动的有限温键向列相。结果提示需重新审视 Na2BaCo(PO4)2、K2Co(SeO3)2 等三角晶格钴酸盐的高场态解释。

Abstract: The spin-1/2 XXZ antiferromagnet on the triangular lattice in a magnetic field is a paradigmatic frustrated quantum magnet, yet its ground-state phase diagram at generic anisotropy and its thermal phase structure remain incompletely understood. Here, using infinite density-matrix renormalization group, infinite projected entangled-pair operator, and exponential tensor renormalization group methods, supported by exact diagonalization, we resolve both issues. At zero temperature, we map the phase diagram continuously from the Ising limit to the Heisenberg point and find that the nonclassical $Ψ$ supersolid is a stable phase just below saturation at all anisotropies studied: it borders the one-third-magnetization up-up-down plateau directly in the strongly easy-axis regime and the semiclassical $V$ phase toward the Heisenberg point. In the strongly easy-axis regime, we uncover an intrinsically finite-temperature bond-nematic phase above the zero-temperature plateau--$Ψ$ boundary, in which the three bond directions become spontaneously inequivalent without transverse spin coherence or three-sublattice order. A hard-core-boson mapping reveals its origin: once thermal fluctuations destroy phase coherence, the field-doped holes settle on domain walls of the local up-up-down order, which are stabilized by quantum kink delocalization and aligned by its $Z_3$ registry. Our results call for reexamining high-field states in triangular-lattice cobaltates such as Na$_2$BaCo(PO$_4$)$_2$ and K$_2$Co(SeO$_3$)$_2$.

### [31] [High-density correlation energy of the electron gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31426)
*Miguel A. L. Marques,Paulo R. Pires,Stefano Pittalis*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文重算了均匀三维电子气关联能高密度展开中 $r_s\ln r_s$ 与 $r_s$ 项系数 $C$、$D$，以及 $r_s^2$ 级单/双对数系数 $E_1,E_2$，给出修正后的 $D=-0.0077880(9)$ Ha。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高密度展开系数 $A,B,C$ 已知精确，但 $D$ 仅有 Carr–Maradudin 的旧估计 $-0.010$ Ha，且该方法存在耦合常数权重问题；同时更高阶对数系数 $E_1,E_2$ 尚需系统核算，以提升关联泛函在高密度极限下的精度。

Method: 采用多体微扰/图论方法，在耦合常数积分下按图类重算：裸环图（bare rings）、含一个一阶交换插入的环图、以及三个不同动量转移的三阶图；对 $r_s^2$ 阶，汇总七项单对数贡献 $E_1$ 与三项长波双对数贡献 $E_2$（后者给出解析闭式）。

Result: 三类图之和为 $-0.0077880(9)$ Ha/电子，比早先 $-0.010$ Ha 高约 0.002 Ha；差异主要源于 Carr–Maradudin 对交换环图的耦合常数权重处理。$E_1=(3.653\pm0.010)\times10^{-3}$ Ha/电子；$E_2=\alpha^2(36\pi^2-208\ln2-221)/(192\pi^4)\approx-1.433\times10^{-4}$ Ha/电子，$\alpha=(4/9\pi)^{1/3}$。现有 $r_s=0.5$ 的扩散蒙特卡洛能量精度不足以区分两种 $D$ 估计。

Conclusion: 更新后的 $D=-0.0077880(9)$ Ha 应替代旧值用于关联泛函高密度展开；$E_1,E_2$ 提供了 $r_s^2$ 阶对数项的精确基准。当前 DMC 数据无法判别 $D$，需更低 $r_s$ 或更高精度计算来验证。

Abstract: The high-density expansion of the correlation energy per electron of the unpolarized three-dimensional electron gas, $ε_c = A\ln r_s + B + C r_s\ln r_s + D r_s + \dots$, is used in many approximations to the correlation functional of density-functional theory. The coefficients $A$, $B$ and $C$ are known exactly. We revisit the three diagram classes used to estimate $D$, namely bare rings, rings with one first-order exchange insertion, and third-order diagrams with three distinct momentum transfers. Their sum is $-0.0077880(9)$ Ha per electron, compared with the earlier estimate of $-0.010$ Ha. The difference comes mainly from the coupling-constant weighting of the exchange rings in the calculation of Carr and Maradudin. At order $r_s^2$, seven contributions to the single-logarithm coefficient $E_1$ sum to $(3.653 \pm 0.010)\times 10^{-3}$ Ha per electron. The three long-wavelength contributions to the double-logarithm coefficient $E_2$ sum to $α^2(36π^2 - 208\ln 2 - 221)/(192π^4) \approx -1.433\times 10^{-4}$ Ha per electron, where $α= (4/9π)^{1/3}$. Available diffusion Monte Carlo energies at $r_s = 0.5$ cannot distinguish between the two estimates of $D$.

</details>


### [32] [A fermionic framework for quantum machine learning benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31458)
*Jérémie Gince,Ayana Sarkar,Stefanos Kourtis*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QML宣称的优势可能并非来自经典不可及的资源，而是架构或归纳偏置的副产品；需要一个可控、可经典模拟且与QML架构对齐的对照体系来甄别。

Method: 以(参数化)量子比特旋转与纠缠门为共享模块，构造自由费米子ML模型（FermiML），覆盖核方法与神经网络、分类与回归；在5个分类数据集31种配置下将QKS/IQP/PQC与费米子替代体（fQKS、fPQC）对比；提出费米子保真度核与Pfaffian协方差核用于回归并建立QML基线；提出可扩展的费米子能量分类器。

Result: fQKS显著优于QKS且与IQP、PQC统计上无差别；fPQC优于IQP与QKS，并在28种配置中与PQC持平；费米子能量分类器在图像上优于主流QML模型，在文本上胜出或接近，整体贴近经典基线。

Conclusion: 性能对架构高度敏感，但费米子替代体能够挑战甚至超越无约束的QML对应模型，且可扩展到真实数据集规模。

Abstract: We present a framework for building and benchmarking scalable machine learning (ML) models based on simulating free-fermion systems. Our fermionic ML (FermiML) framework is (i) versatile enough to encompass kernel methods and neural networks, (ii) applicable to classification and regression, and (iii) scalable to real-world datasets. FermiML models are quantum circuits sharing the building blocks of most quantum ML (QML) models: (parameterized) qubit rotations and entangling gates. This allows us to build fermionic surrogates that mimic the architecture, and hence the quantum resources and inductive bias, of any QML model while remaining efficiently classically simulable. We thus benchmark QML models against close fermionic counterparts on equal footing and ascertain whether a purported QML gain truly stems from classically inaccessible resources or is an accidental side-effect. Across 31 configurations from five classification datasets, we benchmark quantum kitchen sinks (QKS), instantaneous quantum polynomial (IQP) and generic parameterized quantum circuit (PQC) models against fermionic surrogates. fQKS significantly outperforms QKS and is statistically indistinguishable from IQP and PQC; fPQC outperforms IQP and QKS and ties PQC in 28 configurations. We also introduce fermionic fidelity and Pfaffian covariance kernels for regression and establish QML baselines for this harder task. Finally, we introduce a fermionic energy-based classifier scalable to large text and image datasets, which outperforms prominent QML models on images, beats or closely trails them on text, and stays close to classical baselines throughout. Our results show that while performance depends sensitively on architecture, FermiML yields fermionic surrogates that challenge or outperform their unrestricted counterparts and fermionic QML models that match the scale of classical baselines on real-world datasets.

</details>

  in KCoO$_2$ in the $P4/nnm$ structure
  are calculated using a linear response approach, derived from the transverse
  spin susceptibility, integrated over atomic spheres using a projector proportional the local magnetization and including all atoms as magnetic atoms. The exchange interaction between Co and its apical O is found to be by far the largest interaction and ferromagnetic. Its effect on the Néel temperature and the spin wave spectrum are evaluated. The Co-O exchange is found to strongly enhance the critical temperature in the mean field approximation but not in the random phase approximation.

</details>

Result: 各向同性交换（含第十近邻耦合的交替磁劈裂）与中子散射一致；易面各向异性和Γ点能隙由偶极-偶极相互作用主导，使Γ点磁振子劈裂增强约三倍并与实验定量吻合，而DM项虽量值不小却无关紧要；同一哈密顿量重现反节点方向的手性磁振子劈裂（对应电子自旋劈裂），并通过超交换的体积依赖定量解释巨磁体积耦合。

Conclusion: α-MnTe的易面各向异性与磁振子能隙起源是偶极相互作用而非单离子各向异性，DM项不相关；单一微观哈密顿量可统一描述其交换谱、手性磁振子劈裂与巨磁致伸缩。

Abstract: The hexagonal altermagnet $α$-MnTe combines a near-room-temperature collinear order with a strongly spin-split electronic structure, yet the microscopic origin of its easy-plane anisotropy, its zone-center magnon gap and its anomalously large magnetostriction have remained unexplained. We derive the complete low-energy magnetic Hamiltonian of $α$-MnTe from first principles, combining density functional theory and dynamical mean-field theory with a force-theorem evaluation of the full anisotropic intersite exchange, single-ion terms and classical dipolar couplings. The isotropic exchange, including the altermagnetic splitting of the tenth-neighbor coupling, agrees with inelastic neutron scattering data. Contrary to the common single-ion picture, we find that the easy-plane anisotropy and the magnon gap at $Γ$ are governed by classical dipole-dipole interactions, which enhance the zone-center magnon splitting threefold leading to quantitative agreement with experiment, while the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya term is irrelevant despite its size. The same Hamiltonian reproduces the chiral magnon splitting along antinodal directions, mirroring the electronic spin splitting, and quantitatively accounts for the giant magnetovolume coupling of $α$-MnTe through the volume dependence of superexchange.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出自适应含时ghost Gutzwiller近似(td-gGA)，按需增加ghost轨道以高效追踪关联电子长时间实时动力学，在半满Hubbard模型相互作用淬火中与DMFT定量吻合，并在强耦合区将收敛时间延长至DMFT数据的3.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联电子的实时动力学在所有相互作用强度下延展到长时间仍缺乏可靠计算方法；此前固定6个ghost轨道的td-gGA虽能大致重现DMFT结果，但缺少误差估计，无法支撑对外推预测的信心。

Method: 以ghost轨道扩展Gutzwiller近似，构建自适应td-gGA：当动力学过程中积累的关联超过阈值时动态增加ghost轨道数目（每自旋最多至16个），而非固定数目。以Bethe格点半满Hubbard模型的相互作用淬火为测试，和数值精确的非平衡DMFT对比；并用二阶强耦合展开分析长时间振荡频率。

Result: 自适应td-gGA在DMFT数据可及范围内（弱耦合预热区至强耦合collapse-and-revival区）均与DMFT一致；强耦合下收敛时间达DMFT数据的3.5倍。在两类区域交叉附近，系统弛豫到与DMFT一致的熱化值。按ghost轨道数分辨动力学，显示Gutzwiller近似的尖锐动力学转变如何平滑为交叉。延长时间窗口内观测到的振荡频率重整化与二阶强耦合展开预言一致，并给出内建误差估计。

Conclusion: 自适应td-gGA兼具计算效率与系统性可改进性，并提供误差估计，为驱动体系与多轨道关联体系的延长时间动力学提供可行路线，尤其可与张量网络或量子求解器结合用于求解嵌入问题。

Abstract: Following the real-time dynamics of correlated electrons to extended times, at all interaction strengths, remains an open challenge. We address it with an adaptive time-dependent ghost Gutzwiller approximation (td-gGA). The method extends the Gutzwiller approximation by auxiliary ghost orbitals. It adds more of them whenever the correlations that build up during the dynamics exceed a threshold. This is computationally far more efficient than keeping their number fixed. As a demonstration we take the interaction quench of the half-filled Hubbard model on the Bethe lattice. For this paradigmatic problem, numerically exact nonequilibrium dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) serves as the reference. A previous td-gGA study with a fixed set of 6 ghost orbitals per spin largely reproduced the available DMFT data. It lacked, however, an error estimate that would support predictions beyond them. With up to 16 ghost orbitals per spin, adaptive td-gGA agrees with DMFT wherever DMFT data are available, from the weak-coupling prethermal regime to the strong-coupling collapse-and-revival regime. At strong coupling it remains converged up to three and a half times as long as the DMFT data. Near the crossover between the two regimes, the system relaxes to thermal values that agree with DMFT. Resolving the dynamics in the number of ghost orbitals shows how the sharp dynamical transition of the Gutzwiller approximation turns into this smooth crossover. In the extended time window at strong coupling, we observe that the oscillation frequency is renormalized as predicted by our second-order strong-coupling expansion. With its built-in error estimate, adaptive td-gGA offers a systematically improvable route to the extended-time dynamics of driven and multi-orbital correlated systems, especially when combined with tensor-network or quantum solvers for the embedding problem.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验多链耦合体系中E8可积性破缺后出现的超越E8理论的新激发，并为实验（易轴磁性链材料的光谱测量）提供可辨识的判据。

Method: 对弱耦合临界横场Ising链阵列的标度极限哈密顿量采用Hamiltonian truncation精确对角化，系统增大链数并分析低能谱、质量比及偏离临界点（detuning）的标度行为。

Conclusion: E8理论只是多链弱耦合体系的部分描述，新ψ粒子的普适质量比与独特失谐标度可用于在易轴磁性链材料的光谱实验中识别该激发。

Abstract: We study the spectrum of arrays of critical transverse field Ising chains perturbed by a weak inter-chain coupling in the scaling limit, using an exact diagonalization algorithm based on Hamiltonian truncation. As the number of chains is made large, we confirm the existence of excitations below the two particle continuum that can be identified with the celebrated Zamalodchikov $E_8$ theory with small corrections. In addition, we find a distinct $ψ$-particle, with mass $M_\mathrmψ$ which is not contained in the $E_8$ theory. Its ratio to the lowest $E_8$ mass ($M_1$) is a universal number, which we have computed numerically to be $M_\mathrmψ/M_1 = 1.819\cdots$ for a one dimensional array of critical chains. We present evidence that this new state fundamentally involves excitations on neighboring chains, explaining why it has no analogue in the single chain problem. Interestingly the $M_\mathrmψ$ mass has a characteristic scaling dependence on detuning from the critical point that is qualitatively distinct from the $E_8$ masses, which can help identify it in spectroscopic experiments on easy-axis magnetic chain materials.

</details>

Conclusion: 对称性分辨超声是探测化学无序巡游量子临界金属中应变相关电子与磁自由能微小变化的灵敏手段。

Abstract: We investigate the elastic response of cubic NiCoCr$_{0.8}$, a quantum-critical medium-entropy alloy, by high-precision ultrasound velocity measurements. At zero magnetic field, the three symmetrically independent elastic moduli $C_{11}$, $(C_{11}-C_{12})/2=C_t$, and $C_{44}$ monotonically harden upon cooling without pronounced elastic softening or anomalies. In magnetic fields applied along [110], however, a minute but systematic elastic renormalization of the order of $10^{-5}$ is resolved at low temperatures. The tetragonal $E_g$ mode $C_t$ softens with increasing field, whereas the derived bulk $A_{1g}$ mode $C_B$ hardens, revealing an opposite field response between the two strain-symmetry channels. Comparison with the previously reported quantum-critical-Fermi-liquid crossover scale shows that the field-induced elastic renormalization develops already in the quantum-critical regime and evolves continuously across the crossover region, without a sharp elastic anomaly or threshold behavior. These results demonstrate that symmetry-resolved ultrasound provides a sensitive probe of subtle strain-dependent changes in the electronic and magnetic free energy of a chemically disordered itinerant quantum-critical metal.

</details>

Conclusion: 样品形状的全局几何拓扑直接约束并反映在磁化场的性质中；超越等半径管等经典体系、研究非平凡拓扑纳米结构，是连接微分几何拓扑与磁学的重要且具实践价值的研究方向。

Abstract: In this Chapter, we focus on the question of how the global geometric characteristics given by the topology of the sample's shape are reflected in the properties of the magnetization field of the given curvilinear nanoarchitecture. We start with a brief introduction to differential geometry and topology, giving their connection to the specific properties of the vector field represented by the magnetization field defined at the specific manifold. Then we discuss modern fabrication and characterization techniques that make analysis of a sample's topology not only an interesting theoretical concept but also a directly accessible and practically relevant topic. The main attention of the Chapter is dedicated to the theoretical and experimental analysis of nanostructrures with the topology different from the extended films beyond the in-depth studied topic of tubes of the constant radius. In particular, toroidal, spherical, and non-orientable manifolds are considered.

</details>

Abstract: Magnetic phase diagrams of the three antiferromagnetic (AFM) transitions of GdTe$_3$ that occur at zero magnetic field at $T_{N1}=12$ K, $T_{N2}=10.5$ K and $T_1=7$ K are investigated by means of resistance measurements in a magnetic field oriented along the three crystallographic directions. With the magnetic field applied in the basal plane we found re-entrance of an intermediate phase which may be a result of the coupling between the $T_{N2}$ AFM and the charge density wave with possibly a ferroaxial order. A spin flop transition is also observed at a temperature that presumably converges towards a triple point in vicinity of 2.5-3.0 T and $T$ between $T_1$ and $T_{N2}$. Our results help complete a complex phase diagram of GdTe$_3$ and advance a method for investigating magnetic ordering via transport measurements -- a technique applicable to many other compounds.

Method: 在临界点引入只在单点作用的局域场，利用其无关性产生随两倍标度维数衰减的时空调制，使两点关联函数转化为"一个算符进入Boltzmann权重、另一个被测量"的单点测量（局域磁化在任意源强度下关系严格成立）；将该方案植入辅助场量子蒙特卡洛，测量静态自旋磁化率与虚时位移关联，并对半满π-flux Hubbard模型（N_f=2 Gross-Neveu-Heisenberg普适类）做有限尺寸标度分析。

Result: 以空前精度获得静态自旋磁化率与虚时关联；有限尺寸标度给出ν=1.10(3)、η_φ=1.09(4)，与场论预言η_φ≃1接近。由于η_φ>1，临界等时结构因子不随系统尺寸发散，其峰值受背景贡献主导，不能用于提取临界指数；同时相变处自旋动力学强烈非相干，量子Fisher信息保持有界。

Abstract: At criticality, a local field acting at a single point in Euclidean space-time is irrelevant and induces a modulation in space and time that decays with twice its scaling dimension. This turns two-point correlation functions into one-point measurements: one operator insertion enters the Boltzmann weight, the other is measured. For the local magnetization the relation is exact at any source strength. Implemented in auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo, the approach yields the static spin susceptibility and imaginary-time-displaced correlations with unprecedented precision. We apply it to the Dirac-semimetal to antiferromagnetic-Mott-insulator transition of the half-filled $π$-flux Hubbard model. This lattice model provides a regularization of the $N_f=2$ Gross-Neveu-Heisenberg universality class. A finite-size scaling analysis estimates the critical exponents as $ ν=1.10(3)$ and $η_φ=1.09(4)$, close to field-theory predictions of $η_φ\simeq1$. Since $η_φ>1$, the equal-time structure factor at criticality does not diverge with the system size. Hence, the equal-time structure factor at the ordering wave vector is dominated by background contributions and cannot be used for extracting critical exponents. Consistently, the spin dynamics at the transition is strongly incoherent and the quantum Fisher information remains bounded.

Abstract: Resolving the thermodynamic entropy associated with a local impurity in a spatially extended interacting quantum bath remains a numerical challenge. We develop a Local-Maxwell approach that reconstructs the impurity entropy from local observables in worldline quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) simulations. By integrating their temperature responses along the impurity coupling, the method avoids separate estimates of extensive entropies. For even vertex counts, the Maxwell estimator variance remains bounded as the coupling approaches zero. The transverse-field Ising benchmark reproduces exact free fermion results, while an interacting XXZ chain demonstrates up to several hundredfold improvement in statistical efficiency over conventional extensive thermodynamic integration. In a three-channel XXZ impurity junction, we reveal entropy recovery upon cooling and a nonmonotonic coupling dependence of the finite-temperature entropy contrast. These results establish local thermodynamic reconstruction as an efficient approach to impurity entropy in interacting quantum baths.

Abstract: The multichannel fluctuating field (MCFF) approach treats selected collective fluctuations in interacting quantum lattice models nonperturbatively, but has so far been applied only to single-band systems. Here, we extend MCFF theory to multiorbital systems, accounting for the orbital structure of charge and spin instabilities. We apply it to the half-filled two-orbital Kanamori-Hubbard model with repulsive intra-orbital interaction $U$, and both positive and negative Hund's coupling $J$, addressing the competing orders and their interplay. The ability to selectively retain individual fluctuation channels and control their interplay allows us to disentangle their respective contributions and identify the mechanisms underlying competing instabilities. The model features a simple competition between charge density wave and antiferromagnetic fluctuations in the vicinity of $J=U$, and a more intricate competition between antiferromagnetic, orbital, and spin-orbital fluctuations near $J=0$, where the model acquires a higher SU(4) symmetry. The proposed multiorbital extension thus provides a computationally inexpensive and efficient framework for exploring how orbital and spin character shape competing collective fluctuations.

Abstract: Bond-dependent magnetic interactions, strong spin-orbit coupling, and quantum fluctuations in $α$-RuCl$_3$ make it an archetypical candidate for exotic Kitaev quantum spin liquid (QSL) states. While electronic transport, neutron scattering, optical and tunneling spectroscopies were used to probe global QSL signatures in large scale $α$-RuCl$_3$ samples, nanoscale evidence of local QSL behavior via a non-invasive experimental probe is still missing. Here, we use diamond nitrogen-vacancy (NV) spin-noise spectroscopy to probe QSL states in α-RuCl3 nanoflakes with thicknesses ranging from 8 to 150 nm over a temperature range of 4.3-250 K. Our measurements reveal a pronounced decrease in the NV spin-lattice relaxation time ($T_{1}$) below ~100 K in proximity to the $α$-RuCl$_3$ flakes, indicating enhanced GHz-frequency magnetic fluctuations. This temperature scale is consistent with the energy scale associated with Kitaev interactions (~8 meV) and the emergence of low-energy fractionalized excitations. Below 50 K, thicker (> 90 nm) flakes exhibit a distinct bi-exponential $T_{1}$ decay, unlike the single-exponential behavior observed for off-flake and thinner (8 nm) flake measurements, suggesting an increasingly heterogeneous magnetic noise environment. These results establish NV spin-noise spectroscopy as a local, non-invasive probe of low-energy spin dynamics in $α$-RuCl$_3$ nanoflakes and open a route to testing predicted noise signatures of fractionalized excitations in atomically thin QSL candidates.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Finding the ground states of quantum many-body systems remains a central challenge in physics and quantum chemistry. Imaginary-time evolution (ITE) offers a natural, widely used route, but its convergence is typically asymptotic. Variational approaches restrict this evolution to parameterized manifolds, with neural quantum states (NQS) providing flexible representations commonly optimized via stochastic reconfiguration (SR). Their performance depends on the geometry of the variational manifold, the convergence properties of ITE, and the stability of finite-step explicit (forward) integrators. By exploiting local geometric information of the energy landscape, the recently introduced augmented imaginary-time evolution (AITE) can, in principle, reach the ground state in finite imaginary time. To exploit the accelerated convergence of AITE in NQS optimization, we first combine an implicit Euler step with Galerkin projection to derive an implicit SR scheme that permits large, stable steps and substantially outperforms conventional methods. We use it to formulate an implicit version of AITE and show that its acceleration persists under variational projection and finite-step integration with asymptotically quadratic convergence of the energy error. With finite sampling, implicit schemes yield consistent gains, and implicit AITE becomes increasingly advantageous.

</details>

Abstract: This paper provides a theoretical justification for using the Causal-Conditional Schrödinger-Newton theory (CCSN) as a phenomenological model for classical gravity sourced by quantum matter. In CCSN, the gravitational field is driven not by expectation values in the global quantum state, but by conditional expectation values of the stress-energy tensor evaluated on local states determined by measurement records in the causal past. Because these local states are defined operationally from measurement records, the formulation is independent of any particular interpretation of quantum measurement while leading to unambiguous phenomenological dynamics. We apply this framework to a self-gravitating optomechanical system under continuous measurement and show that the resulting stochastic Schrödinger evolution, augmented by a classical gravitational field sourced by local conditional stress-energy expectations, can be made consistent with energy-momentum conservation by introducing a corrector tensor. The corrector restores the conservation law without altering the leading Newtonian predictions of CCSN, contributing only negligible post-Newtonian corrections in current experimental regimes. These results place CCSN on firmer theoretical footing and clarify its role as a viable phenomenological model for ongoing and future tests of classical gravity coupled to quantum matter.

We then discuss de Broglie-Bohm theory and the Madelung formulation, which provide a framework for comparing guided trajectories with ensemble descriptions involving momentum fluctuations. This correspondence is established for Gaussian ensembles and potentials at most quadratic.

Abstract: Indefinite causal order allows processes in which the order of events is not predetermined, enabling information-processing advantages unattainable with fixed causal structures. A long-standing open problem is whether such processes can be certified without trusting the devices used to demonstrate them, analogous to device-independent certifications of Bell nonlocality. Causal inequalities offer a route towards this goal, since their violation is intended to rule out all definite causal structures. However, fully device-independent certification of indefinite causal order remains elusive. The difficulty lies in the closed laboratories assumption, which requires each party to interact at most once with systems that mediate communication. Without this assumption, ordinary two-way communication can reproduce causal-inequality violations without involving indefinite causal order. Yet an untrusted device could violate the assumption by interacting with the mediating system more than once, while remaining undetected from input-output statistics alone. We address this loophole by embedding a minimal set of additional experimental components into a certification scheme. These elements probabilistically probe the underlying process, enabling the closed laboratories assumption to be validated directly within the protocol. Our framework provides operationally meaningful minimal-assumption tests of indefinite causal order, clarifying its distinction from nonlocality and paving the way for cryptographic and self-testing applications.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Entanglement and magic are usually regarded as independent quantum resources. We introduce the spinal reflected entropy, defined on a neighborhood of a spine of the underlying surface as the reflected entropy between a disk around the vertex of the spine and bands along its noncontractible loops. It obeys an area law with a topological correction and contains one additional term that carries all the dependence on the stored state. On a torus, this term equals the stabilizer Rényi entropy of the stored state plus the logarithm of the ground-state degeneracy. The mechanism is topological. On the surface sewn to its reflected copy, the bands close into tubes, logical Pauli operators become anyon charges threading them, and these charges are distributed as the outcomes of Bell sampling of the stored state. Abstract: We develop a framework for preparing and transporting coherent quantum samples of nonreversible Markov chains. We study two complementary constructions with different oracle requirements and target states. The first uses coherent access to both forward and time-reversed transitions and targets the stationary quantum sample, with a filtering cost governed by a multiplicative-reversibilization gap. The second requires only forward transitions and selects the leading eigenstate of a flat discriminant, which may differ from the stationary quantum sample; we quantify this bias under additional spectral and reversibility-on-average conditions. We establish a common transport procedure and derive error and resource bounds that account for spectral properties, overlaps between successive selected states, and the costs of coherent multi-step transition oracles. Up to logarithmic factors, spectral-filter degrees scale inversely with the square roots of the relevant spectral gaps, while transport introduces an additional inverse-square-root dependence on certified lower bounds on squared overlaps between successive selected states. </details>

Abstract: Quantum amplitude estimation can reduce the number of evaluations needed to estimate an average, but its advantage in PDE uncertainty quantification depends on the cost of each coherent solve. We then introduce a Sobolev--Riesz preconditioner and prove that the resulting condition number is bounded by $α_{\max}/α_{\min}$, independently of the grid resolution. We construct block encodings and reversible-arithmetic circuits for this formulation and show how to estimate the mean kinetic energy without reconstructing the full velocity field. By accounting for both sampling and solver costs, we establish conditions under which the method has a gate-complexity advantage over single-level classical grid-resolving solvers. Numerical experiments verify the conditioning bound and the accuracy of the circuit components at the tested sizes. The complexity advantage is conditional on the stated input and discretization assumptions; no quantum-hardware runtime advantage is demonstrated.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Decoherence, which arises from the interaction of a quantum system with a typically much larger thermal environment, or bath, poses major challenges to the controllability of quantum systems. The dynamics of such open quantum systems is often represented by Markovian quantum master equations of the Lindblad or Redfield type. These equations describe the dynamics of the open system with a sum of coherent (Hamiltonian) and decoherent (dissipative) contributions. It is often assumed that coherent controls only impact the coherent contribution, while the decoherent contribution is taken to be time- and control-independent. However, the decoherent contribution must also be control-dependent if coupling to a thermal bath is to relax the system to a Gibbs state at thermal equilibrium, which is given in terms of the system's Hamiltonian. In this paper, we investigate the effect of accounting for control-dependent decoherence on coherent control protocols. To this end, we examine the effect of accounting for control-dependent decoherence on the controllability of an open quantum system in the case of a two-level system coupled to a thermal bath. We show that coherent controls wield greater influence on the dynamics of the system than predicted by a Lindblad-type equation that models decoherence as time- and control-independent.

Abstract: We extend the semiclassical formalism of Dunlap--Kenkre (1986) and of Mamani et al. (2017) to obtain the effective Hamiltonian of a rectangular tight-binding lattice with interactions to all neighbors, in the presence of a rapidly oscillating field $\mathbf f(ωt)$ and a static potential $V(x,y)$, both arbitrary, by means of: a canonical transformation, the semiclassical approximation and the Kapitza-type time-averaging techniques (Mamani 2017). (2002), and we show that the resulting band-collapse is in general anisotropic --axial interactions are suppressed but some diagonal interactions persist--, so that exact dynamic localization in the stroboscopic sense is guaranteed only when the band-collapse is complete for all neighbors. This effort will position DOE to transition to larger systems: production-scale quantum computers that comprise 1,000 to 10,000 logical qubits, perform 1 to 10 billion hard logical operations, and execute scientifically useful computations at scale. The Blueprint calls for transformative R&D and collective action to accelerate the advent of scientific quantum utility and bring it within reach by 2028.

Result: 最佳配置将最大单次运行压缩3.87倍，总时空体积为基线的8.79倍（预算内）；同一核心上Ekerå折衷仅达2.81倍。该最大运行比Initio Intelligence的所有设计至少小2.6倍。563个预注册模拟试验全部成功。

To gain more, we make each exponent bit cheaper with windows that share one table point among exponent values related by six symmetries of the curve, on a point-addition core borrowed from the ECDSA.Fail leaderboard. One random scalar and one random start per run give every approximate addition uniform operands, and a bound on each run's outcomes certifies, for every key, a decoder that discards flagged runs. We count every Toffoli gate and every qubit of every run, and each configuration solved all 563 of its pre-registered simulated trials on secp256k1. The best one shrinks the largest run 3.87 times at 8.79 times the baseline's total volume, against 2.81 times for Ekerå's tradeoff on the same core, and its run is at least 2.6 times smaller than every design of Initio Intelligence.

</details>

*Yicheng Guang,Yuliang Xiong,Xun Gao,Yueqi Chen*

TL;DR: 提出Duet——一种面向含横向门的逻辑Clifford电路的神经解码器，将"错误经门传播的固定路径"和"逻辑结果线性传播"等已知结构内建到网络中，只学习各层实际发生的错误，并与搜索解码器Tesseract级联，在精度与速度上均优于匹配解码及现有神经解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离子阱与中性原子平台上，横向门只需一轮综合征测量，比格点手术快至多d倍；但横向CNOT会在两个码块间复制错误，迫使解码器联合读取整个电路的所有码块。算法解码器精度高但慢，神经网络解码器缓解了该权衡，却仍从数据中重新学习本已精确已知的门间错误传播与逻辑结果传播规律，浪费样本与算力。

Method: Duet包含三点设计：(1) 每个横向门把错误移到固定位置，故网络仅在门所连接的位点间交换特征（稀疏连接）；(2) 逻辑结果经Clifford门精确线性传播，网络只预测每层错误引起的翻转，再用soft XOR逐层合并；(3) 电路由少数几种门构成，故以每层的门序列作为输入，每个码距只需一个网络即可解码不同规模与深度的电路。外层与搜索解码器Tesseract级联，仅对网络置信度最低的少量采样重新解码。

Result: 在2~4个码块、最多16层的通用电路上，仅用Duet即在各码距上精度均超过匹配解码；配合Tesseract，在d=9时失败率比LoMatching低44倍。在Lukin组的随机电路上，d=7时失败率为其神经解码器的0.299倍。整体保持高速：每层耗时2.4~121.7 μs，在所有码距下均低于其网络单个门的耗时。

Conclusion: 将解码器设计中已知的电路结构（门诱导的错误置换与Clifford逻辑线性传播）显式编码进网络，并辅以少量高置信度筛选后的搜索解码，可在横向门架构上同时获得更低的逻辑错误率与更高的解码吞吐。

Abstract: On neutral-atom and trapped-ion machines, a transversal gate needs only one syndrome round, so a logical gate takes up to d times less time than with lattice surgery. However, transversal gates make decoding harder, because a transversal CNOT copies errors between two blocks of physical qubits, so a decoder must read all blocks of a circuit together. Algorithmic decoders for such circuits gain accuracy at the cost of speed. Neural decoders ease this trade-off, but they must infer from data where errors go across each gate and how logical outcomes propagate through the circuit, although both are known exactly. In this work, we present Duet, a neural decoder for logical Clifford circuits with transversal gates that builds in this known information and learns only which errors occurred. Duet leverages three insights. First, each transversal gate moves an error to a fixed place, so the network exchanges features only between the sites that a gate connects. Second, a logical outcome propagates through Clifford gates exactly and linearly, so the network predicts only the flips that the errors of each layer cause and combines the layers by a soft XOR. Third, every circuit is built from the same few gates, so the network takes the gates of each layer as input and one network per code distance decodes circuits of different sizes and depths. We pair Duet with the search decoder Tesseract, which re-decodes the few shots on which the network is least confident. On general circuits with 2 to 4 blocks and up to 16 layers, Duet alone is already more accurate than matching at every distance, and with Tesseract it fails 44 times less often than LoMatching at distance 9. On the random circuits of the Lukin group, it fails 0.299 times as often as their neural decoder at distance 7. The composition stays fast and takes 2.4 to 121.7 μs per layer, less than their network takes per gate at every distance.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 以Hamming方案与Krawtchouk多项式为工具，研究{0,1}^d上与对称随机游走（转移概率仅依赖Hamming距离）关联的连续与离散时间量子游走。连续时间情形利用特征值互异性导出分布形式；离散时间情形对简单随机游走、二项式权重随机游走、独立随机游走分别推导时间平均分布的显式标度极限。

Result: 连续时间游走：特征值互异时，Hamming距离的时间平均分布为离散反正弦律。离散时间游走的标度极限分别为：简单随机游走→反正弦律；二项式权重游走→不同支撑上反正弦律的等权混合；独立随机游走→在0与1/2处各具质量1/2的原子分布。

Conclusion: 连续与离散时间模型仅在简单随机游走情形下极限一致，在二项式权重与独立随机游走情形下不同，说明二者之间的对应关系不具有普适性。

Abstract: We study continuous-time and discrete-time quantum walks on the hypercube $\{0,1\}^d$ associated with symmetric random walks, that is, random walks whose transition probabilities depend only on the Hamming distance, using the Hamming scheme and Krawtchouk polynomials. For continuous-time walks, we show that the time-averaged distribution of the Hamming distance is a discrete arcsine law whenever the eigenvalues are distinct. For discrete-time walks, exact formulas for the time-averaged distribution are known as combinatorial sums or as mixtures involving Krawtchouk polynomials, but their scaling limits as $d\to\infty$ (time average first) have not been specified. We obtain them explicitly for three random walks: the simple random walk (an arcsine law), the random walk with binomial weights (an equal mixture of arcsine laws with different supports), and the independent random walk (atoms of mass $1/2$ at $0$ and at $1/2$). Comparing with the continuous-time limits, the two models agree for the simple random walk but differ for the other two, so the correspondence between them is not universal.

### [63] [Probing the inner structure of bound states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31763)
*Yahui Chai,Yibin Guo,Karl Jansen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文利用量子子空间展开（QSE）构造复合粒子本征态，通过比较不同算符集合所得态与全QSE态之间的保真度，量化各类算符在态中的重要性，从而揭示复合粒子的内部结构，并以大质量Schwinger模型的标量介子为基准进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望建立一种系统方法，将复合粒子（如介子）的本征态构造与其内部结构及可观测性质联系起来；QSE提供了一种可按算符类型逐步扩展试探空间、从而分辨各组成部分贡献的工具。

Abstract: While quantum state exclusion has received considerable attention, the analogous task of measurement outcome exclusion remains less explored. Random exclusion profiles provide a statistical description in terms of optimized hypothesis-testing errors and are monotone under both types of processing. This connects outcome exclusion with statistical comparison in the spirit of Blackwell, linking the ability to suppress measurement outcomes to the possibility of simulating one measurement from another.

</details>

Abstract: Simulating the dynamics of interacting fermions remains a central computational challenge across physics and chemistry. The formalism combines a compact representation with explicit expressions for interacting gates and exact treatment of number-conserving fermionic Gaussian and Clifford unitaries. Abstract: While chirality is known to impact chemical and optical properties of molecular systems, as well as their biological function, far less is known about its impact on quantum coherence. Abstract: We describe an architecture and workflow for learning and generalizing the syntax and parameters of holographic quantum circuits (HQCs), which are natural quantum circuit analogs of classical tensor networks. An HQC uses mid-circuit measurements and resets on a small subset of "physical" qubits whose measurement outcomes become correlated through coupling with a register of persistent "ancilla" qubits, analogous to the physical and bond indices of a tensor network. Hence, an HQC with fixed qubit resources can efficiently generate samples from a large quantum state, and adding qubits to the HQC ansatz has the effect of exponentially increasing the effective bond dimension. Our machine learning approach provides mechanisms for harnessing both features of HQCs by extrapolating the syntax (e.g., a sequence of gates, measurements, and resets) and parameters (e.g., the angles appearing in single-qubit rotations) from a dataset of HQCs to longer or more expressive HQCs. We demonstrate the methodology and its performance on an analytically solvable dataset formed by a vector-valued generalization of the W-state. Finally, we provide an outlook for usage of the methodology for scaling the effective bond dimensions of HQCs in quantum simulation.

Eight labels suffice to preserve all feasible pairs of interface choice probabilities, and this bound is tight. The known classical four-label bound yields minimum-cost saturation. With an unrestricted label budget, the minimum source cost is zero if the target can be reproduced by a purely classical protocol, and one otherwise. For Werner states with identical orthogonal measurement pairs, the exact minimum source cost is obtained for every label budget. The three-label cost jumps at visibility $\frac{2}{3}$. Four labels give zero cost at every visibility. In a family of separable states, the two-label cost varies nonmonotonically with local bias while the full correlation tensor remains fixed.
