  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三正交性等正交型条件是qubit及素数维CSS码中横向非Clifford门构造的主要代数筛选准则。已有所有正例中证书都伴随非Clifford逻辑门，因此悬而未决的问题是：是否存在一个CSS码族，能满足任意高阶正交性条件却不含此类门。作者要判断正交性证书是否为可靠的判据，还是可能给出假阳性。

Method: 研究对象为有限阿贝尔群G（exp(G)|p−1，p≥5素数）上由特征标取值构造的对称谱CSS码Q(A)=CSS(C(A),C(A))。核心机制是"对偶码的Schur饱和"恒等式C(A)^⊥ * C(A)^⊥ = F_p^G，它由自正交条件A∩(−A)=∅推出，并湮灭所有次数≥3的局部多项式相位。作者给出完整的门群分类S^{CSS}_{≤p−1}(A)=C(A)^⊥ ⊕ C(Stab(A))，并说明判据可推广到满足C_Z^⊥*C_Z^⊥=F_p^n的一般CSS码对，同时对[[58,16,3]]_5这一LDPC实例做了计算验证。

Result: 得到"Clifford天花板"结构界：任何严格保持CSS的局部对角横向门必位于Clifford层级≤2层。在布尔环面码族中假阳性最为尖锐：码对ℓ≤p−2满足ℓ-正交性，但非Clifford扇区坍缩为零。代表性实例[[64,56,3]]_5编码56个逻辑ququint，码率R=0.875、距离3，通过全部正交性筛选测试，却不含任何次数的局部对角非Clifford门；唯一非Pauli幸存者是均匀二次驱动Q_u，它容错、逻辑非平凡、处于Clifford第2层，但不携带任何magic态资源。

Conclusion: 正交型条件在对称谱CSS码中不是充分判据：它能被满足到任意高阶而门集依然不含非Clifford元素，因此会系统性产生假阳性。需以Schur饱和恒等式或门群分类等更强的结构判据替代单纯的ℓ-正交性检验，才能可靠判定横向non-Clifford门的存在性。

Abstract: Orthogonality-type conditions (triorthogonality in the qubit setting and its prime-dimensional analogues), are the primary algebraic screening criterion for transversal non-Clifford gate constructions in CSS codes. In every known positive construction the certificate is accompanied by a non-Clifford logical gate, leaving open whether a CSS family can satisfy these conditions to arbitrarily high order while admitting no such gate. We answer this question for the class of symmetric spectral CSS codes $Q(A)=CSS(C(A),C(A))$ built from character evaluation on a finite abelian group $G$ with $\exp(G)\mid p-1$, $p\geq 5$ prime. The mechanism is the identity \[C(A)^\perp * C(A)^\perp=\mathbb{F}_p^G,\] which we call Schur saturation of the dual code. It follows from the CSS self-orthogonality condition $A\cap(-A)=\varnothing$ and annihilates every local polynomial phase of degree $\geq 3$. The complete gate-group classification $S^{CSS}_{\leq p-1}(A)=C(A)^\perp \oplus C(Stab(A))$ shows that every strictly CSS-preserving local-diagonal transversal gate lies at level $\leq 2$ of the Clifford hierarchy, a structural bound we call the Clifford ceiling. The criterion extends to arbitrary CSS pairs satisfying $C_Z^\perp * C_Z^\perp=\mathbb{F}_p^n$; the LDPC instance $[[58,16,3]]_5$ is verified computationally. The false-positive character of the orthogonality certificates is sharpest in the Boolean toric family: these codes satisfy $\ell$-orthogonality for $\ell\leq p-2$, yet the non-Clifford sector collapses to zero. The flagship instance $[[64,56,3]]_5$ encodes $56$ logical ququints at rate $R=0.875$ with distance-$3$ protection, passes every orthogonality screening test, and yet admits no local-diagonal non-Clifford gate of any degree. The unique non-Pauli survivor is the uniform quadratic drive $Q_u$, fault-tolerant, logically nontrivial, and Clifford at level $2$, carrying no magic-state resource.

</details>

Abstract: Recent advances in AI-assisted theoretical research suggest that AI agents can contribute to research-level problems. To address this question, we build a simulated quantum optics laboratory and integrate it with an AI agent in QuOptLab. The laboratory includes optical instruments, a robotic arm, and cameras, enabling the agent to carry out and iteratively refine experiments. We consider two complementary tasks: thin-film thickness measurement in free-space optics and edge-coupler design in integrated photonics. In the thin-film thickness measurement task, the agent repeatedly analyzes measurement results and revises its measurement method, including calibration and data analysis, reducing thickness-estimation error from 7.13 to 0.066~nm$^2$ over six epochs in a classical-interference run. For edge-coupler design, the agent iteratively proposes structural modifications, evaluates the resulting coupling performance, and uses the feedback to guide subsequent revisions, increasing simulated coupling efficiency from 85.81% to 91.98%.

Abstract: We study gradient-based search for a class of convex simulation optimization problems with gradients estimated from noisy outputs. With independent outputs, the optimal classical convergence rate for the expected objective gap is of order $O(N^{-2/3})$, where $N$ is the query budget. We apply quantum mean estimation to paired finite differences and progressively increase their estimation accuracy as the perturbations shrink, improving the convergence rate to $O(N^{-4/3})$. Furthermore, we show that coupling nearby evaluations improves the classical and quantum convergence rates to $O(N^{-2/(3-κ)})$ and $O(N^{-4/(3-κ)})$, respectively, where $κ\in[0,1]$ describes the variance reduction in paired outputs. Thus, quantum estimation halves the accuracy exponent in query complexity across coupling orders. Moreover, we establish matching lower bounds and show that these accuracy exponents are minimax optimal for fixed decision dimension, even when query locations and batch sizes are adaptive. Reconstruction from feasible differences retains the exponents when every simulation query must be feasible and accommodates boundary optima.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数同时估计受互补性与测量不相容性本质限制，SLD-Cramér-Rao界无法饱和，制约量子测量器件灵敏度。

Method: 研究参数编码动力学保持反平行探针构型的条件；刻画满足该要求的量子信道，给出此类构型存在且能在不损失量子Fisher信息前提下饱和SLD-CRB的条件；对量子比特证明优势存在当且仅当编码信道为unital；推广至双探针有限维qudit，考察导数反转与转置映射探针态及更一般的多探针构型。

Abstract: The simultaneous estimation of multiple parameters is fundamentally constrained by complementarity and measurement incompatibility, which limits the sensitivity of quantum measurement devices by preventing the symmetric logarithmic derivative (SLD) Cramér-Rao bound (CRB) from being saturated. We show that antiparallel probe configurations can overcome this limitation when the dynamics of the parameter encoding preserve the said configurations. We characterise the quantum channels that satisfy this requirement and establish conditions under which such configurations exist and can saturate the SLD-CRB without compromising the quantum Fisher information, leading to a metrological advantage. For qubit probes, we prove that this advantage is present if and only if the encoding channel is unital. We extend this analysis to the two probe, finite dimensional qudit case by considering derivative reversal and transpose map based probe states, as well as more general multiprobe configurations. For each of these cases, we identify conditions under which an advantage exists. Our results establish connections between channel symmetries, probe configurations and the attainability of multiparameter precision limits.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典量子态合并的 $S(A|B)_\rho$ 结果建立在精确 i.i.d. 假设上，而实际场景中源常有结构偏差。作者希望回答：这一成本在最坏情形意义下对源类是否稳健，即是否存在单一通用协议覆盖整个源类（而非针对每个源的定制协议）。

Result: 对任意 $R>S(A|B)_\rho$，单一协议序列对每一弱几乎 i.i.d. 源及所有足够大块长 $n$ 实现误差趋零、净成本 $\le nR$ ebits；由于各类均含精确 i.i.d. 源，$S(A|B)_\rho$ 在该类内对通用合并是可达到且下界紧的（最优）。更一般地，只要置换平均源的两副本边缘在迹范数下收敛到 $\rho_{AB}^{\otimes 2}$ 即可保证误差趋零。

Conclusion: 量子态合并的渐近净纠缠成本对 i.i.d. 偏差具鲁棒性，且可被单一通用协议实现对整类源的最优保证；两副本边缘的迹范数收敛是通用可合并性的自然充分判据。

Abstract: In quantum state merging, Alice aims to transfer her share of a bipartite state to Bob while preserving the joint state with a purifying reference. For i.i.d. copies of $ρ_{AB}$, the optimal asymptotic net entanglement cost is $S(A|B)_ρ$ ebits per pair. We show that this cost is robust under deviations from exact i.i.d. structure. For every $R>S(A|B)_ρ$, a single sequence of one-way LOCC protocols, depending only on $ρ_{AB}$ and $R$, merges every weakly almost i.i.d. source along $ρ_{AB}$ with vanishing error. The net entanglement cost is at most $nR$ ebits for all sufficiently large blocklengths $n$. This guarantee also covers the Wasserstein and Mazzola--Sutter--Renner subclasses. Each class contains the exact i.i.d. source, so $S(A|B)_ρ$ is optimal for universal merging within each class. More generally, the same protocols achieve vanishing error whenever the two-pair marginal of the permutation-averaged source converges in trace norm to $ρ_{AB}^{\otimes 2}$.

</details>


### [77] [Validity and maximum violation of the entanglement-assisted quantum Singleton bound for linear stabilizer codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32126)
*Jae-Hyun Baek,Byung-Sun Won,Jon-Lark Kim*

TL;DR: 本文确定了线性纠缠辅助稳定子码在何种条件下满足纠缠辅助量子Singleton界、失效程度有多大：若恢复逻辑qudit所需的qudit数（加上纠缠对接收端半部分）小于2k则界成立；等于2k时界最多高估长度不超过该维最长经典MDS码长度减维数；大于2k时违背量由给定Singleton亏量的经典码长度刻画，并在一个逻辑qudit时达到界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brun–Devetak–Hsieh的纠缠辅助量子Singleton界是估计纠缠对消耗量的标准工具，但已知在大距离处存在违背该界的码；需要精确刻画对线性纠缠辅助稳定子码（含所有qubit此类稳定子码）该界何时成立、可失效多远。

Abstract: Entanglement-assisted quantum codes protect quantum information with entangled pairs shared in advance, and the entanglement-assisted quantum Singleton bound of Brun, Devetak and Hsieh is the standard estimate of how many pairs they consume. Codes that violate it at large distance are known. For linear entanglement-assisted stabilizer codes, which include all qubit stabilizer codes of this kind, we determine when the bound holds and how far it can fail. The answer depends on how many qudits, together with the receiver's halves of the pairs, recover the logical qudits. If this number is less than twice the number of logical qudits, the bound holds, also for degenerate codes. If it is exactly twice that, the bound overstates the number of pairs by at most the length of the longest classical maximum distance separable (MDS) code of that dimension, minus the dimension. Codes carrying values and derivatives of polynomials on the projective line attain this maximum whenever the field size is at least that dimension and the MDS conjecture is known for it, which includes every prime and, by a recently announced result, every even field size. If the number is more than twice the number of logical qudits, the violation is bounded in terms of the lengths of classical codes of a given Singleton defect, and the bound is attained for one logical qudit. Apart from cited MDS lengths, the proofs are formalized in the Lean proof assistant.

</details>


### [78] [Transfer tensor description of driven inhomogeneous quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32189)
*Ferdos Fessahaye,Rajesh Dutta,Michelle Anderson,Chern Chuang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将转移张量法（TTM）推广到时间非齐次与周期驱动体系，提出双时转移张量层级T(n,k)与"时间均化"概念，并用于分析非RWA驱动的二能级系综与Dunlap–Kenkre条件下的驱动紧束缚链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准TTM依赖动力学映射的时间平移不变性（TTI），而含时驱动或相干控制会破坏TTI，使TTM无法直接外推这类体系的开放系统动力学。因此需要把TTM推广到时间非齐次与周期驱动情形。

Method: 引入双时转移张量层级T(n,k)。对周期t0的驱动，采用亚频闪采样Δt=t0/Nc，得到块状TTI：T(n+mNc,k+mNc)=T(n,k)，只需学习Nc个起始时刻分支；对驱动相位（起始时刻）取平均，即"时间均化"，可恢复单指标TTI。随后在两类体系中检验：(i) 超越RWA的非均匀展宽二能级系综；(ii) Dunlap–Kenkre条件下的驱动紧束缚链，并定义广义出射赝流。

Result: (i) 相位诱导记忆正比于Rabi耦合平方，无序诱导记忆正比于非均匀展宽平方；相位诱导记忆具规范（参考系）依赖性——在驱动旋转帧中仅源于反旋转项并在RWA下消失，而在实验室帧中RWA之后仍大体保留，且两者记忆非可加地耦合。(ii) 干净链在频闪意义下被冻结；亚频闪张量显示动力学局域化由记忆核中延迟的、与周期可公度的重聚焦所强制，与Anderson局域化的即时回流形成对比；广义出射赝流刻画了扩散对无序的非单调依赖：中等无序破坏重聚焦使粒子扩散到更大但有限的区域，强无序则使其局域。

Conclusion: 双时TTM层级与时间均化框架为周期驱动开放系统提供了可行的短时轨迹外推方案，并揭示了相位诱导记忆与无序诱导记忆的竞争、以及驱动体系中延迟重聚焦机制区别于静态Anderson局域化的本质特征。

Abstract: The transfer tensor method (TTM) extrapolates open quantum system dynamics from short-time trajectories by encoding memory effects in a discrete hierarchy of transfer tensors. Standard TTM relies on time-translational invariance (TTI) of the dynamical maps, which is broken by time-dependent driving or coherent control. We generalize TTM to time-inhomogeneous and periodically driven systems with a two-time transfer tensor hierarchy $\mathcal{T}(n,k)$. For driving with period $t_0$, sub-stroboscopic sampling ($Δt = t_0/N_c$) restores a block TTI, $\mathcal{T}(n+mN_c, k+mN_c) = \mathcal{T}(n,k)$, so that only $N_c$ starting-time branches must be learned. Averaging over the driving phase (or start time), termed temporal homogenization, restores single-index TTI at the price of phase-induced memory that combines non-additively with that of static disorder. We study two systems. (i) In an inhomogeneously broadened two-level ensemble driven beyond the rotating-wave approximation (RWA), the phase-induced memory scales with the square of the Rabi coupling and the disorder-induced memory with the square of the inhomogeneous width. The phase-induced memory is frame dependent: in the drive's rotating frame it arises solely from counter-rotating terms and vanishes under the RWA, whereas in the lab frame it largely survives the RWA. (ii) In a driven tight-binding chain at the Dunlap--Kenkre condition, the clean chain is stroboscopically frozen, whereas sub-stroboscopic tensors show that dynamical localization is enforced by delayed, period-commensurate refocusing in the memory kernel, in contrast to the immediate backflow of Anderson localization. A generalized outgoing pseudoflux tracks the non-monotonic dependence of the spreading on disorder: moderate disorder breaks the refocusing and lets the particle spread over a larger but finite region, whereas strong disorder confines it.

</details>

Abstract: Local discriminability and purification are two key axioms in an information-theoretic axiomatization of quantum mechanics as proposed by G. M. D'Ariano et al. (Quantum Theory from First Principles, 2017). Both of them refer to compound systems, the theory of which is rather directly assumed to be given by the tensor product structure. This structure, though well understood, should, however, be based on a more careful study of formal composition rules describing how local effects can be embedded into the compound effect space and how to reduce joint states onto subsystems. In that we follow the earlier work of T. Matolcsi, (Acta Sci. Math., 1975), and D. Aerts and I. Daubechies, (Helvetica Physica Acta, 1978), as summarized, e.g., in The Logic of Quantum Mechanics, 1981, by E. Beltrametti and G. Cassinelli. We also establish axioms of local discrimination stating that compound states can be told apart by local measurements the notion of which we also formally define. The purification axioms first introduced as axioms of quantum mechanics by D'Ariano et al. are also rewritten in a weaker form which we believe could be fruitful for future axiomatizations. These reformulations bring us closer to proper tensor product structures for joint state and effect spaces, so that the embedding map for states factors through the projective tensor product of the local state spaces and the embedding map for effects factors through the injective tensor product of the local effect spaces. We also discuss the case of multipartite composition axioms and associativity properties required in these settings.

Result: 在固定安全目标下，量子通信需求降低1～2个数量级（取决于具体参数）；在较高量子比特错误率下收益更显著，信道最大可容忍错误率由约3.5%提升至约9%。代价是ZKP引入显著计算开销，估计明显超过协议本身的量子获取（acquisition）时间这一通常的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: ZKP是收紧QOT安全性界、降低量子通信开销的有效手段，并显著提升协议对信道噪声的容忍度；通过可调变体可在保留可观效率增益的前提下精确控制计算开销，但其整体实用价值仍取决于ZKP计算成本与量子通信成本之间的平衡。

Abstract: While quantum protocols for oblivious transfer can be built from weaker assumptions than classical ones, the current protocols remain highly impractical due to stringent security bounds that demand a large amount of quantum communication. In this work, we propose an enhancement to quantum oblivious transfer protocols by leveraging zero-knowledge proofs (ZKPs). At a high level, we modify the protocol such that the receiver proves that they are following honestly some crucial steps of the protocols, without revealing extra information to the sender. This yields a tighter security bound against a malicious receiver. For practical scenarios with a fixed security target, this approach reduces the amount of required quantum communication by one to two orders of magnitude, depending on the specific parameters. Furthermore, the benefits of our approach become particularly pronounced at higher qubit error rates, and our proposal even extends the maximum tolerable error rate of the quantum channel from approximately 3.5% to about 9%. The ZKP, however, introduces a substantial computational overhead, estimated to significantly exceed the acquisition time of the protocol, which is usually the primary bottleneck. To address this limitation, we also introduce a variant that serves as a tunable trade-off between the protocol with and without our modification. This enables precise adjustment of the computational overhead while still retaining significant, albeit moderately reduced, efficiency gains.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rydberg阻塞天然排斥使二值目标最优的构型，如何把这一约束性惩罚转化为可用的资源，并在有限惩罚条件下保持基态能量，是本文的出发点。

Method: 把有限的阻塞惩罚展开为加权排除图集合，整个集合保持基态能量不变；每张图上保留的相互作用用于设定该布局的局部失谐。以两个耦合方格的模拟为分析模型，考察反转目标耦合对单点能量斜率与残余相互作用集体模的影响；并在32个留出的十二比特目标上，以128次采样统计基态恢复概率，比较残余失谐与调优均匀脉冲两种设置，最后对池化样本按原目标打分。

Result: 反转目标耦合不改变均匀占据下的单点能量斜率，但残余相互作用的集体模使偏好对角从对齐转为相反。32个留出十二比特目标上，128次采样的模拟基态恢复概率：残余失谐平均69.82%，调优均匀脉冲平均24.31%。

Conclusion: 残余失谐编码了阻塞展开中的集体相互作用，是提升采样效率的关键自由度；将多个原生布局的样本池化后按原目标评分，可搜索任何单一布局都无法实现的哈密顿量基态。

Abstract: Rydberg blockade can exclude the configuration that minimizes a binary objective. We expand its finite penalties into a weighted ensemble of exclusion graphs. The full ensemble preserves the ground energy, and the interactions remaining on each graph set its local detunings. In simulations of two coupled squares, reversing a target coupling leaves the single-site energy slopes at uniform occupation unchanged, yet a collective mode of the residual interactions switches the favored diagonals from aligned to opposed. On 32 held-out twelve-site targets, the simulated probability of recovering a ground state in 128 shots averages 69.82\% with residual detunings and 24.31\% with a tuned uniform pulse. By scoring the pooled samples under the original objective, we can use these native layouts to search for ground states of a Hamiltonian that none implements alone.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出首个全脉冲变分自编码器QSVAE（基于量子启发的QLIF神经元），在LHC双喷注潜空间中展示其对噪声扰动的鲁棒性显著优于经典、脉冲与量子模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC上模型无关的新物理搜索依赖无监督异常检测，量子与神经形态探测器是潜在方案；但需要一个可微分、可训练且对噪声鲁棒的量子-神经形态检测器。

Method: 将量子启发漏积分放电（QLIF）神经元由基于测量的电路重构为可微分计算单元，据此构建全脉冲变分自编码器QSVAE，并在LHC双喷注潜空间中与经典、脉冲、量子模型对比基准；另设周期性经典对照模型复现QLIF效应，定位到sin²膜映射，并辅以消融实验刻画量子-神经形态模型空间。

Conclusion: sin²膜映射是一种内生正则化器，使鲁棒性与由量子比特态空间提供的模型容量解耦。

Abstract: Model-agnostic searches for new physics at the Large Hadron Collider utilise unsupervised anomaly detection, where quantum and neuromorphic detectors represent potential approaches. We develop the first fully spiking variational autoencoder, a quantum-inspired spiking variational autoencoder (QSVAE), built on the quantum-inspired leaky integrate-and-fire (QLIF) neuron. In this work we recast the neuron from a measurement-based circuit to a differentiable computational unit. This neuron is benchmarked against other classical, spiking, and quantum models on an LHC di-jet latent space. We show that under noise perturbations the QLIF network retains $0.95$ of its clean accuracy, while accuracy retention for twelve classical models falls to $0.74$-$0.85$. The QLIF anomaly scores respond $3.5$-fold less to the noise perturbation than any of the control model's scores. We compare against a periodic classical control that reproduces the QLIF effect, isolating it to the $\sin^2$ membrane map. This membrane map is a native regulariser that decouples robustness from model capacity supplied by the qubit state space. We demonstrate this through several benchmarks and ablation studies to characterise the performance landscape of the quantum-neuromorphic model space.

### [83] [Quantum Coherence and Basis-Dependent Correlations in Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32358)

Result: 在Tsallis相对熵相干度量下，块对角乘积态的基依赖关联为零，且特殊情形下"关联为零"与块对角乘积结构等价；在量子Fisher信息相干度量下，块对角乘积态同样给出零基依赖关联，而二比特情形下零化条件对应更宽泛的"类块对角"乘积结构。

Abstract: Quantum coherence and quantum correlations are two fundamental features of quantum systems and are closely related in various quantum information tasks. In general, due to the existence of correlations, the amount of coherence in a bipartite state should be no less than that in the reduced states. We focus on what types of states have an equal amount of coherence in the global state and the local states. For such states, the basis-dependent correlations (the difference between the coherence of a bipartite state and its reduced state with respect to a set of standard orthogonal local bases) vanish. Based on the Tsallis relative entropy coherence measure, we prove that block-diagonal product states exhibit vanishing basis-dependent correlations. Furthermore, we establish an equivalence between the absence of basis-dependent correlations and the block-diagonal product structure in a special case. We then consider the coherence measure based on quantum Fisher information and show that block?diagonal
  product states again lead to vanishing basis-dependent correlations, while in the two-qubit case the vanishing condition constrains the state to a broader block-diagonal-like product structure. These results provide a structural characterization of bipartite states with vanishing basis-dependent correlations under different coherence measures.

</details>


### [84] [Thermal state preparation with shallow quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32388)
*Cenk Tüysüz,Simon Deconihout,Enrico Rinaldi,Michele Grossi*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在含噪量子硬件上制备热态要求电路在低温下仍保持浅层；即使已有精确的经典态表示，将其编译为合适电路代价也可能很高。

Method: PP-VarQITE：以 Pauli 传播（Pauling propagation）模拟虚时演化并配合变分量子虚时演化（VarQITE），全部参数在经典侧确定；采用固定电路架构，将目标温度编码于角度中，从而避免量子侧优化。

Result: 在 Ising 与阻挫 Heisenberg 模型上，最多 100 量子比特可复原低温能量与关联；在阻挫链与二维晶格上，电路深度约为序列张量网络编译所得最浅电路的 1/2～1/10；在 Quantinuum Helios 离子上用 96 和 98 量子比特制备 48 格阻挫链与 7×7 Ising 晶格，晶格电路最多 42 个双比特层，并复现从高温到低温的二聚化序与全局关联的发展。

Conclusion: 经典侧完成电路设计，可在超出态矢量模拟实际能力的电路规模上实现低温热态制备。

Abstract: Preparing thermal states on noisy quantum hardware requires circuits that remain shallow at low temperatures. Even when accurate classical state representations are available, compiling them into suitable circuits can be costly. We introduce PP-VarQITE, combining Pauli propagation with variational quantum imaginary-time evolution to determine circuit parameters entirely classically, with target temperatures encoded in the angles of a fixed circuit architecture. In numerical studies of Ising and frustrated Heisenberg models, we recover low-temperature energies and correlations using up to 100 qubits. Across the frustrated chain and two-dimensional lattice, our circuits are approximately two to ten times shallower than the lowest-depth circuits from sequential tensor-network compilation. These shallow circuits enable us to prepare a 48-site frustrated chain and a $7\times7$ Ising lattice using 96 and 98 qubits on the Quantinuum Helios trapped-ion quantum processor. We reproduce the development of dimer order and lattice-wide correlations from high to low temperatures, with the lattice circuits requiring at most 42 two-qubit layers. Through classical circuit design, we demonstrate low-temperature state preparation at circuit sizes beyond the practical reach of statevector simulation.

</details>


### [85] [Second-order asymptotics for the quantum and private capacities of the erasure channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32395)
*Mark M. Wilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了擦除信道在有限分组、给定不保真度下传输量子/私密经典信息的最优二阶编码率，并给出匹配的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 擦除信道的一阶容量已知，但有限分组下（二阶）精确的编码率修正尚未给出严格匹配的上下界，尤其私密经典通信的二阶行为及其对隐私判据的依赖性不明确。

Method: 对输入维度 d≥2、擦除概率 p<1/2、固定不保真度 ε∈(0,1)，给出码本基-2对数规模 n(1-2p)log₂d + 2√(np(1-p))log₂d·Φ⁻¹(ε) + O((ln n)²)。证明通过对单码在所有同规模损失模式上的平均性能作界，结合正确/秘密通信检验、张量积平均与一个经典多项式恒等式；允许任意混合编码器与集体解码器。

Result: 所得高斯项同时适用于纠缠传输、纠缠生成与私密经典通信，并对任意固定不保真度（含 ε 超过 1/2）证明匹配上界，余项为 O((ln n)²)。若将理想环境态固定为实际边缘态，分位数变为 Φ⁻¹(1-√(1-ε))。

Conclusion: 擦除信道的二阶编码率由二项涨落决定；对每条消息均要求私密误差界可保持原高斯项，而将理想环境态固定为实际边缘态会在二阶改变结果，说明隐私判据的选择对有限分组通信有实质影响。

Abstract: An erasure channel either delivers a quantum system intact or tells the receiver that it was lost. How much quantum information, or secret classical information, can a finite block transmit without assistance? We determine the optimal second-order coding rates for input dimension $d\ge2$, erasure probability $0<p<1/2$, and fixed infidelity (one minus fidelity) $0<\varepsilon<1$. For $n$ channel uses, the optimal base-two logarithmic code size is \begin{equation}\tag{A}\label{eq:abstract-rate} n(1-2p)\log_2d +2\sqrt{np(1-p)}\log_2d\,Φ^{-1}(\varepsilon) +O((\ln n)^2), \end{equation} where $Φ$ is the standard normal cumulative distribution function and $Φ^{-1}$ is its quantile function. The square-root term is the second-order correction. This formula holds for entanglement transmission, entanglement generation, and private classical communication. Quantum error measures failure to preserve entanglement. Private error compares the actual output with a correctly decoded uniform message independent of an optimized environment state. The achievable Gaussian term and a matching quantum expansion below infidelity one half were established recently. We prove matching upper bounds for every fixed infidelity, including private communication, with the displayed remainder. The proof bounds one code's average performance across all loss patterns of a given size; binomial fluctuations then determine the Gaussian correction. It combines tests of correct and secret communication with tensor-product averaging and a classical polynomial identity. Arbitrary mixed encoders and collective decoders are allowed. Requiring the private error bound for every message preserves the Gaussian term. Fixing the ideal environment state to the actual marginal instead changes the quantile to $Φ^{-1}(1-\sqrt{1-\varepsilon})$. Thus the precise privacy criterion affects communication at second order.

</details>


### [86] [Electron spin relaxation and coherence times of silicon vacancies in 4H-SiC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32451)
*Irene Donà,Michele Segantini,Gianluca Marcozzi,Nguyen Tien Son,Takeshi Ohshima,Ha Duong Ngo,Boris Naydenov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文用EPR研究了4H-SiC中S=3/2的硅空位色心V1、V2（及未知色心X）在80–300 K下的自旋性质，测定自旋浓度并测得卫星谱线的T1、T2长于中心谱线，指出退相干主要源于核自旋与电子自旋扩散。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金刚石、SiC等晶体中的色心自旋是量子比特与量子传感器的候选体系，其中SiC与现有半导体工艺兼容而尤具吸引力；但4H-SiC中硅空位色心（V1、V2）的电子自旋浓度、相干与弛豫性质的系统表征仍不充分。

Method: 采用电子顺磁共振（EPR）测量4H-SiC中电子自旋S=3/2的硅空位色心V1、V2，温度范围T=80–300 K；谱图包含中心线与卫星线，中心线来自V1、V2及未知色心X。通过连续波EPR分析定量自旋浓度，并在中心线与卫星线上分别测量自旋相干时间T2与弛豫时间T1。

Result: 自旋浓度分别为c_V1=1.47±0.3×10^14、c_V2=5.6±1.12×10^13、c_X=1.03±0.21×10^13 spins/cm³。卫星跃迁的T1、T2均长于中心跃迁，在T=80 K处最大值分别为42.8±1.3 ms（T1）和0.708±0.004 ms（T2）。退相干主要来源于核自旋扩散与电子自旋扩散。

Conclusion: 4H-SiC中V1、V2硅空位色心具有可量化的自旋浓度与毫秒量级弛豫/相干时间，且卫星跃迁因受自旋扩散影响较弱而表现出更优的相干性能，提示可通过选择跃迁谱线优化该类色心的量子应用性能。

Abstract: Color defect centers carrying electron spins in solid crystals like diamond and silicon carbide (SiC) are promising candidates for qubits and quantum sensors, with SiC material being particularly interesting due to its compatibility with the existing semiconductor technology. Here we investigated the electron spin properties of silicon vacancy centers (V1 and V2) both electron spin $S = 3/2$ in 4H-SiC in the temperature range $T=80-300$~K using electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR). The EPR spectrum shows central and satellite lines, where the former ones originate from V1 and V2 centers, as well as from unknown defect center X. The analysis of continuous wave EPR measurements revealed a spin concentration of $c_{\mathrm{V1}}=1.47\pm0.3\times10^{14}$, $c_{\mathrm{V2}}=5.6\pm1.12\times10^{13}$ and $c_{\mathrm{X}}=1.03\pm0.21\times10^{13}$ spins/cm$^{3}$. Additionally, electron spin coherence $T_2$ and relaxation times $T_1$ have been measured on the central and on the satellite spectral lines, resulting in longer $T_1$ and $T_2$ for the satellite transition, compared to the central one, with maximum values of $42.8\pm1.3$~ms and $0.708\pm0.004$~ms respectively at $T=80$~K. We reveal, that decoherence is mainly caused by nuclear and electron spin diffusion.

</details>


### [87] [Machine Learning-Based Quantum Error Mitigation for Bound-State Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32459)
*Sumit Choudhary,Tarunpreet Kaur,Harjeet Kaur,Harpreet Singh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出结合残差学习与凸投影的机器学习量子误差缓解框架，保证重构密度矩阵物理有效，并在IBM硬件上验证三/四量子比特超核模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子器件噪声严重影响可靠量子模拟；现有ML-QEM性能依赖训练数据集，且重构的量子态可能不满足物理有效性（如正定性、迹为1）。

Method: 在ML-QEM中引入残差学习与凸投影，将重构密度矩阵约束到物理有效集合；系统研究不同训练数据集（Haar随机态、VQE拟设态）与回归模型（岭回归、核岭回归等）的影响；与常规ML-QEM、读出误差缓解、零噪声外推对比；在IBM量子硬件上对三量子比特Λ-氘核与四量子比特Λ-α超核体系进行测试。

Result: 三量子比特体系中，基于VQE拟设态训练的岭回归给出基态能量-0.134 MeV（理想-0.135 MeV），态保真度0.999；四量子比特体系中，核岭回归给出-3.014与-3.045 MeV，保真度0.995与0.998。

Conclusion: 物理约束的重构结合训练数据集与回归模型的合理选择，可提升所研究量子模拟任务的ML-QEM性能。

Abstract: Noise in quantum devices remains a major challenge for reliable quantum simulations, making quantum error mitigation an important tool for improving their accuracy. Machine learning-based quantum error mitigation (ML-QEM) provides a useful approach, but its performance depends on the training datasets and may produce reconstructed quantum states that are not physically valid. In this work, we introduce an ML-QEM framework that uses residual learning with convex projection to ensure that reconstructed density matrices satisfy physically valid density matrices. We study the effect of different training datasets and regression models using Haar-random and variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) ansatz states. We compare the proposed approach with conventional ML-QEM, readout error mitigation, and zero-noise extrapolation. The method is tested on IBM quantum hardware for three- and four-qubit simulations of the bound $Λ$-deuteron and $Λ$-$α$ hypernuclear systems, respectively. For the three-qubit system, ridge regression trained on VQE-ansatz states gives a ground-state energy of -0.134 MeV, close to the ideal value of -0.135 MeV, with a state fidelity of 0.999. For four-qubit system, kernel ridge regression gives ground-state energies of -3.014 and -3.045 MeV, with corresponding state fidelities of 0.995 and 0.998. These results show that physically constrained reconstruction, along with the choice of training datasets and regression models, can improve ML-QEM performance for the quantum simulations studied in this work.

</details>


### [88] [When Randomness Hides Quantum Simulation Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32497)
*Yue Cao,Tianfeng Feng,Jonathan Allcock,Chenfeng Cao,Qi Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Trotter误差在可观测值上的抑制程度如何依赖输入态的随机性：证明state 2-design的平均绝对可观测误差上界可比1-design的紧界在qubit数上指数级更小，用excess frame potential刻画1-design到2-design的过渡，并给出帧势按1/T衰减的哈密顿轨迹下p阶Trotter数O(T^{1+3/(4p)})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Trotter公式以离散步近似量子动力学，但实测可观测值的误差往往远小于最坏情形估计。作者希望厘清这种误差抑制与输入态随机程度之间的关系，从而为Trotter误差的"可见性"提供理论刻画与保证。

Method: 引入state k-design（1-design、2-design）刻画输入态随机性；给出state 2-design的平均绝对可观测误差界，并与1-design的紧界比较；用excess frame potential量化系综的二阶矩距离，以frame potential（成对态重叠度量）描述偏离随机态统计的程度；进一步分析帧势先按1/T衰减后饱和的哈密顿轨迹，推导p阶Trotter数。

Result: state 2-design的平均绝对可观测误差界在qubit数上可比1-design的紧界指数级更小；通过frame potential与excess frame potential可定量刻画从1-design到2-design的误差过渡，并识别出对均方误差偏差最敏感的可及可观测值；对帧势按1/T衰减再饱和的轨迹，得到保证精度所需的p阶Trotter数上界O(T^{1+3/(4p)})（T为演化时间）。

Conclusion: 将输入态统计与Trotter误差的可观测性联系起来，为实用量子模拟提供理论保证与基础。

Abstract: Trotter formulas approximate quantum dynamics by discrete steps, but errors in measured observables can be much smaller than worst-case estimates. We investigate how this suppression depends on the degree of randomness in the input states. We show that the mean absolute observable-error bound for state 2-designs can be exponentially smaller in qubit number than the tight bound for state 1-designs. To characterize this error transition from 1-design to 2-design, we use the excess frame potential to quantify the ensemble's second-moment distance. We quantify departures from these random-state statistics through the frame potential, a measure of pairwise state overlaps, and identify the accessible observables most sensitive to the resulting deviations in mean-square error. Extending the analysis to a Hamiltonian trajectory whose frame potential decays as $1/T$ before saturation, we also obtain a sufficient $p$th-order Trotter number $\mathcal O(T^{1+3/(4p)})$ for evolution time $T$. These findings connect input-state statistics to the visibility of Trotter errors, giving a theoretical guarantee and foundation for practical quantum simulation.

</details>


### [89] [The infinite-dimensional extended Bloch representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32498)
*Massimiliano Sassoli de Bianchi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在扩展Bloch表示中，作者用重整化（广义Bloch球半径按N增长）解决了N→∞时Born概率对应单纯形膜体积消失的问题，并证明该增长是唯一给出非退化极限的方式；极限膜为有限维膜的投影极限，且（重整化后）是唯一与Born规则相容的分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Bloch表示下，N结果测量的Born概率等于单纯形膜上N个区域的相对体积；但N→∞时体积趋于零、均匀膜集中于单位球中心，导致无法定义极限概率。作者希望找到一个自然的重整化方式，使大N极限下测量相互作用的分布仍然非退化、并与Born规则一致。

Method: 采用重整化方法：令广义Bloch球半径随N线性增长，考察测量相互作用点到单纯形各面的距离。证明只有该增长速度使这些距离成为独立的指数随机变量并给出非退化极限。以有限维膜的投影极限构造极限膜，并分析纯点谱（区域变为锥、状态粒子趋于无穷、膜塌缩线平行）与连续谱（进一步放大得到Poisson点过程及概率密度形式的Born规则）两种情形；同时证明有限维中均匀膜是唯一与Born规则相容的膜，并把机制解释为独立指数时钟之间的竞争（race）。

Result: 半径按N增长是唯一能给出非退化极限的增长；重整化后，测量相互作用到单纯形各面的距离为独立指数随机变量；极限膜（不偏向任何结果）是有限维膜的投影极限，且是唯一与Born规则相容的分布。纯点谱下膜区域化为锥、状态趋于无穷远、塌缩线趋于平行；连续谱下进一步放大得到Poisson点过程，并给出概率密度的Born规则。另外证明均匀膜在有限维中是唯一与Born规则相容的膜，整个机制在各维度都可解读为独立指数时钟的竞争。

Conclusion: 大N极限下的Born概率可由重整化后的极限膜严格定义：该膜是有限维膜的投影极限，且为唯一与Born规则相容的分布；测量过程可视为独立指数时钟的竞争。这为扩展Bloch表示提供了一个自洽的、不依赖于偏爱某些结果的无穷维推广。

Abstract: In the extended Bloch representation (EBR) of quantum mechanics, the Born probabilities of a measurement with N outcomes are the relative volumes of N regions of a simplex-shaped abstract membrane, whose points represent the "hidden" measurement interactions. However, when N tends to infinity, the volumes vanish and the uniform membrane concentrates at the center of the unit ball. We show that this problem can be solved by renormalization: if the radius of the generalized Bloch ball grows like N, the distances of the measurement interactions from the facets of the simplex become independent exponential random variables. This is the only growth with a non-degenerate limit, and the limit membrane, which cannot live in the limit of the generalized Bloch space without privileging some of the outcomes, is the projective limit of the finite-dimensional membranes and, once renormalized, the only distribution compatible with the Born rule. For a pure point spectrum, the regions of the membrane become cones, the particle representing the state recedes to infinity, and the lines along which the membrane collapses become parallel. For a continuous spectrum, a further magnification yields a Poisson point process and the Born rule for probability densities. We also show that the uniform membrane is the only one compatible with the Born rule in finite dimension, and that the mechanism can be read, in every dimension, as a race between independent exponential clocks.

</details>


### [90] [Controlled teleportation of an unknown qutrit and its control power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32511)
*Xiao-Xu Li,Feng-Li Yan,Ting Gao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于非最大纠缠信道、局域维度不等的受控qutrit量子隐形传态协议，给出与输入态无关的成功概率，并以保真度差定义控制力，找到同时优化控制力与成功概率的控制器测量基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在局域维度不等的条件下研究受控量子隐形传态中控制器的作用，特别是如何量化控制器的控制权限，并弄清控制器测量基的选择对协议性能的影响。

Method: 构造任意未知qutrit的受控隐形传态协议：Charlie持有一个qubit，Alice与Bob各持有一个四能级粒子，信道为非最大纠缠；接收方借助辅助qubit以概率方式、单位保真度恢复输入态。推导总成功概率；在保持发送方联合测量固定、对所有纯qutrit输入态均匀平均并包含所有测量结果的条件下，求有/无控制器合作时的最优平均隐形传态保真度解析式，以其差值定义控制力；对给定共享态搜索控制器测量基。

Result: 总成功概率与输入态无关；得到有/无控制器合作时最优平均保真度的解析表达式；对文中所考察的共享态，确定了能同时最大化控制力与隐形传态成功概率的控制器测量基。

Conclusion: 厘清了局域维度不等情形下控制器测量在受控隐形传态中的作用，并给出兼顾控制力与成功概率的测量基选择方案。

Abstract: We propose a controlled quantum teleportation protocol for an arbitrary unknown qutrit using a nonmaximally entangled channel, with Charlie holding a qubit and Alice and Bob each holding a four-level particle. With the aid of an auxiliary qubit, the receiver can recover the input state probabilistically with unit fidelity. We derive the overall success probability and show that it is independent of the input state. To quantify the controller's authority, we obtain analytical expressions for the optimal average teleportation fidelities with and without the controller's cooperation, keeping the sender's joint measurement fixed and averaging uniformly over pure qutrit input states with all measurement outcomes included. Their difference defines the control power of the protocol. For the shared states considered here, we determine a controller measurement basis that simultaneously maximizes the control power and the teleportation success probability for a given shared state. These results clarify the role of the controller's measurement in controlled teleportation with unequal local dimensions.

</details>


### [91] [Quantum Capacity with Incoherent Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32514)
*Oxana Shaya,Zahra Baghali Khanian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在编码端限制为非相干操作时，任意弱的去极化噪声都使量子容量在每个输入基下归零；单量子比特亦不可靠传输。给出非相干编码下容量的单字母公式，并证明非相干解码至多使容量降低一倍（因子最优），同时界定了非相干解码器的保真度并计算多量子比特Pauli信道的容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 明确非相干（incoherent）资源限制如何影响量子信道容量：以往容量结果多假设编码/解码不受对称性约束，本文关注当编码或解码被限制在固定基下的非相干操作时，容量如何退化，以及退化的程度是否可由简单因子刻画。

Method: 以去极化信道为典型模型，分析非相干编码下的纠缠保真度上界（每块长度下 ≤ 1−ε/2）；在解码不受限时推导非相干编码容量的单字母公式——对具有相同环境输出的基态系综取Holevo信息最大值；给出非相干解码器保真度界；对多量子比特Pauli信道计算非相干编码容量，并构造达到因子2下界的例子。

Result: 非相干编码下，任意弱去极化噪声即使容量为零，单量子比特也无法可靠传输，纠缠保真度上界为 1−ε/2；解码不受限时容量有单字母表达式（相同环境输出基态系综的最大Holevo信息）；无论编码是否相干，非相干解码至多使容量降低为原来的1/2，且该因子最优；得到多量子比特Pauli信道在非相干编码下的容量值。

Conclusion: 基相干性（coherence）是量子通信容量的关键资源：去掉编码端相干性会使容量对噪声极度脆弱，而解码端相干性的损失仅造成常数因子（2倍）的容量损失，且该损失不可改进。

Abstract: Arbitrarily weak depolarizing noise drives the quantum capacity to zero in every input basis when the encoder is restricted to incoherent operations. Even one qubit cannot then be sent reliably: if the output is replaced by the maximally mixed state with probability $\varepsilon\le1/2$, the entanglement fidelity is at most $1-\varepsilon/2$ at every block length. With unrestricted decoding, a single-letter formula gives the capacity with incoherent encoding as the maximum Holevo information over ensembles of basis states with identical environment outputs. For either incoherent or unrestricted encoding, imposing incoherent decoding reduces the capacity by at most a factor of two; this factor is optimal. We bound the fidelity of incoherent decoders and evaluate the capacities with incoherent encoding for multiqubit Pauli channels.

</details>


### [92] [Optimal Trace Distance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32530)
*Ziyun Chen,Minbo Gao,Qisheng Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出样本复杂度最优的迹距离估计器：以 Θ(d²/(ε²log²d·max{1,ε²log²d})) 的每态样本量将两个未知 d 维量子态的迹距离估计到加性误差 ε，达到 Wang (2026) 的下界；方法是层析成像与多项式近似的结合，并用量子傅里叶分析给出方差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迹距离是量子态区分、性质检验与量子学习中的核心量，但以往估计方案在样本复杂度上是否最优尚不明确；Wang (2026) 给出了下界，本文目标是在上界侧给出与之匹配的最优估计器，闭合该问题的复杂度刻画。

Method: 估计器由两部分组成：先用量子态层析（tomography）获取态的有限精度经典描述，再通过多项式近似构造迹距离的有效估计量；关键技术是用量子傅里叶分析（quantum Fourier analysis）导出估计量的方差上界，从而完成样本复杂度分析。

Result: 对任意两个未知 d 维量子态，以 Θ(d²/(ε²log²d·max{1, ε²log²d})) 的每态副本数即可将迹距离的加性误差控制在 ε 以内，且该上界与 Wang (2026) 的下界相匹配，故样本复杂度在阶上最优。注意该表达式隐含两种区制：当 ε²log²d ≥ 1 时退化为 Θ(d²/log²d) 的样本量；否则为 Θ(d²/(ε⁴log⁴d))。

Conclusion: 迹距离估计的样本复杂度被完全确定（达到阶最优），显示对数因子可从朴素层析方案中被系统性去除；层析与多项式近近似＋傅里叶方差分析的组合是解决此类估计问题的有效范式。摘要未给出常数因子与计算复杂度细节，实际可实现性仍需进一步考量。

Abstract: We present a trace distance estimator with optimal sample complexity. Specifically, the trace distance between two unknown $d$-dimensional quantum states can be estimated to within additive error $\varepsilon$ using \[ Θ\left(\frac{d^2}{\varepsilon^2\log^2(d)\max\left\{1,\varepsilon^2\log^2(d)\right\}}\right) \] samples of each quantum state. This matches the lower bound of Wang (2026). The estimator combines tomography and polynomial approximation, with variance bounds from quantum Fourier analysis.

</details>


### [93] [Quantum optimization beyond qubit count by low-energy subspace sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32560)
*Mateo Alonso,Gonzalo Blázquez-Gil,Pablo Díez-Valle*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子子空间采样下降(QSSD)启发式算法，将问题变量数与量子比特数解耦，用固定角QAOA作低能采样器，在IBM 152比特设备上处理10000变量问题，100次线路执行即达0.95以上近似比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字量子器件采用"一变量一比特"编码，使量子优化算法只能处理经典求解器已表现优异的中小规模问题，限制了量子优化实用价值。

Method: QSSD为迭代式启发式：每轮从全变量空间中采样子空间，用固定角度（预计算）的QAOA作为低能态采样器进行能量下降；因角度固定，每轮仅需一次线路执行，且保持对问题全局结构的知识。在高阶无约束二进制优化(HUBO)与Max-cut实例上测试，使用IBM Heron与Nighthawk设备（最多152比特）。

Result: 在10000变量的最大实例上，仅100次线路执行即达到>0.95的近似比。与贪心、分支定界、模拟退火等精确及启发式经典基线对比，QSSD仍未超越最佳经典基线，但在固定预算下运行时间随问题规模的扩展更缓慢。

Conclusion: QSSD是可落地的组合优化启发式，指向大规模问题中量子子空间采样可能具备竞争力的区间；硬件改进可进一步提升性能，推动量子优化迈向实用性。

Abstract: On current digital quantum devices, standard one-qubit-per-variable encodings restrict quantum optimization algorithms to problem sizes where classical solvers typically excel. To avoid this constraint, we introduce Quantum Subspace Sampling Descent (QSSD), an iterative heuristic that decouples the number of problem variables from qubit count, while preserving knowledge of the problem's global structure. QSSD drives the energy descent using a fixed-angle Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm as a low-energy sampler. As the angles are precomputed, each iteration requires just a single circuit execution. We test QSSD on IBM Heron and Nighthawk quantum devices with up to 152 qubits on instances of higher-order unconstrained binary optimization and Max-cut problems. On the largest instance with 10000 variables, QSSD reaches an approximation ratio above 0.95 with 100 circuit executions. We provide both exact and heuristic classical baselines, namely greedy, branch-and-bound solver, and simulated annealing. While our approach still does not match the performance of the best classical baseline, it shows promise in a slower runtime scaling with problem size given a fixed budget. This suggests a regime of large problems where subspace sampling on quantum devices could become competitive, as we also demonstrate how improvements in hardware will lead to better algorithm performance. QSSD is thus a practical heuristic for combinatorial optimization problems that helps bring quantum optimization a step closer to utility.

</details>


### [94] [Phase information and measurement in the Stochastic--Quantum correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32578)
*Jason Doukas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究随机-量子对应中量子"提升"的非对角自由度（预测信息）如何编码于随机描述，提出"情境依赖随机过程族"概念，并说明其续延概率可编码该预测信息、可据此从构型概率重构中间提升量子态，同时澄清量子测量中"主动续延"与"物理记录"的分工，证明忠实随机实现者至少存在一个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机-量子对应表明一般随机过程可与量子动力学建立联系，随机转移概率可在希尔伯特空间上得到相容表示（量子提升）。然而，量子提升中非对角自由度所承载的预测信息在随机描述中如何被编码尚不清楚，需要给出随机侧的可观测载体。

Method: 引入"情境依赖随机过程族"（family of context-dependent stochastic processes）这一概念，考察其续延概率（continuation probabilities）；通过选取合适的续延，仅利用系统构型概率即可完全重构中间提升量子态；并以此框架分析量子测量，将基依赖的量子概率区分为"主动续延"（使量子概率成为可及的构型概率）与"物理记录相互作用"（记录实际构型并引入测量扰动）。

Result: 续延概率确实编码了量子提升的非对角预测信息，合适的续延能由构型概率完整重构中间提升量子态。仅凭根量子概率并不能保证理想化记录相互作用的每个随机实现者都忠实记录系统测量前构型，但可证明总存在至少一个忠实随机实现者。

Conclusion: 预测信息在随机侧以情境依赖过程族的续延概率形式存在，量子测量的"概率可及性"与"记录/扰动"可由两个不同机制承担；量子概率本身不足以保证记录的忠实性，但忠实实现者的存在性可被保证。

Abstract: The recently proposed stochastic-quantum correspondence establishes a connection between general stochastic processes and quantum dynamics, in which stochastic transition probabilities admit compatible representations on a Hilbert space, known as quantum lifts. We ask how the predictive information represented by off-diagonal degrees of freedom in the quantum lift is encoded within the stochastic description. We introduce the notion of a family of context-dependent stochastic processes and show that their continuation probabilities encode this predictive information. In particular, suitable continuations allow the intermediate lifted quantum state to be reconstructed entirely from system configuration probabilities. This perspective also clarifies the nature of quantum measurement: active continuations make basis-dependent quantum probabilities accessible as configuration probabilities, while a separate physical interaction records the realized configuration and introduces measurement disturbance. Although the rooted quantum probabilities alone do not guarantee that every stochastic realizer of the idealized recording interaction faithfully records the system's pre-measurement configuration, we show that at least one faithful stochastic realizer always exists.

</details>


### [95] [Unbounded GHZ dimension from fixed-order multiphoton sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32673)
*Heng Li,Xizhi Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过将光子源输出的三条路径视为一致超图的边，把"每输出路径恰一光子"的接受事件与完美匹配对应，构造出边不相交完美匹配数任意多且无其他匹配的三元超图，从而证明三光子源可产生局部维度任意大的GHZ态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究基于"每输出路径一光子"筛选事件所产生GHZ态的局部维度上界：已知双光子源情形局部维度受限（偶数路径数大于4时最大为2），需要判定增加单源光子数能否突破该限制，并给出构造性刻画。

Method: 将每个相干源馈入的三条路径视为3-一致超图的一条边，使被接受的发射模式与完美匹配一一对应；据此构造具有任意多（每个标签一个）边不相交完美匹配、且不存在其他完美匹配的3-一致超图；并将构造推广到任意固定r≥3的r-一致超图。

Result: 双光子源：当路径数为大于4的偶数时，最大局部维度为2；三光子源：存在可实现任意大局部维度GHZ态的构造；推广至r≥3后，当路径数取r的正整数倍时，GHZ维度随路径数至少呈多项式增长。

Conclusion: 源的内部标签数与超图完美匹配结构共同决定GHZ局部维度；三光子及以上多光子源可突破双光子源的维度瓶颈，且维度增长由超图匹配数控制，为高维GHZ态制备提供组合设计途径。

Abstract: We study the local dimension of Greenberger--Horne--Zeilinger (GHZ) states obtained by selecting events with one photon per output path. Each coherent source emits photons with a fixed internal label into a distinct set of paths, and the sets for each label partition the outputs. For two-photon sources, the maximum local dimension is 2 for every even number of paths greater than 4. However, we show that three-photon sources can produce GHZ states of arbitrarily large local dimension. To prove this, we view the three paths fed by each source as an edge of a $3$-uniform hypergraph, so that accepted emission patterns correspond exactly to perfect matchings. We construct such hypergraphs with arbitrarily many edge-disjoint perfect matchings, one per label, and no others. The construction extends to $r$-uniform hypergraphs for every fixed $r\ge3$, yielding a GHZ dimension that grows at least polynomially as the number of paths ranges over the positive multiples of $r$.

</details>


### [96] [Observation of programmable spatiotemporal quantum-inspired bits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32689)
*Long Chen,Zi Xuan Cai,Qing Chun Yin,Zhou He,Zhi Cai Yu,Xiaohan Cui,Tie Jun Cui,Jian Wei You*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在空时超表面上实现可编程的量子启发式比特：每偏振通道由四个离散响应相干合成出具有纯量子比特投影几何的两分量态，并可同时组织为可重构斯托克斯天空子及不同偏振态的多谐波输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干叠加可将信息编码于物理态几何，但单独制备各个态并不能确定其在波中的组织方式，波传播与调制对干涉构成约束，因此需要将局部态制备与场的整体组织联系起来。

Method: 构建空时超表面，对每个偏振通道的四个离散响应进行相干合成，生成两分量态（纯量子比特投影几何）；采用联合态空间合成，在保持共享波形干涉约束的同时使不同偏振态共存于不同分辨频率；用一对共享编码驱动空间与时间编码。

Result: 实现按预设轨迹排布的可重构斯托克斯天空子，并在48个实验可分辨的非零谐波级次上产生不同偏振态，仅由一对共享编码对生成。

Conclusion: 将局部态制备与可编程场组织相连接，为可借助几何路径、空间绕数与协同谱态进行编码的量子启发式信息载体奠定物理基础。

Abstract: Coherent superposition allows information to be encoded in the geometry of physical states1-7. Preparing individual states, however, does not determine how they can be organized throughout a wave, because the wave propagation and modulation constrain the underlying interference. Here we observe programmable spatiotemporal quantum-inspired bits in a space-time metasurface. Coherent synthesis from four discrete responses per polarization channel produces two-component states with the projective geometry of a pure quantum bit. Joint state-space synthesis retains the interference constraints of the shared waveform while allowing distinct polarization states to coexist at different resolved frequencies. Paired spatial and temporal codes organize these states into the prescribed trajectories, reconfigurable Stokes skyrmions and distinct polarizations at 48 experimentally resolved nonzero harmonic orders generated by a shared code pair. This connects the local state preparation to programmable field organization, establishing a physical basis for quantum-inspired information carriers whose encoding can exploit the geometrical paths, spatial winding and coordinated spectral states.

</details>


### [97] [Quantum Community Detection for Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32738)
*Vicente P. Soloviev,Brian Coyle,Stefano Scali,Michal Krompiec*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子谱社区检测器（deteQt、projeQt）在特征选择与聚类流程中的表现：将deteQt推广至多社区递归二分，并提出基于量子奇异值变换谱滤波的projeQt，一次性提取多个社区，在MNIST上与传统社区检测方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区检测可作为特征选择与聚类的替代方案，而用量子算法检测社区有望带来量子加速；但既有量子谱社区检测器多只能二分、仅利用低阶特征向量，需要评估其在真实任务流程中的适用性。

Method: 1) 将模块度矩阵算法deteQt通过递归二分推广到多社区；2) 提出projeQt：利用量子奇异值变换（QSVT）对编码的模块度矩阵施加谱滤波，保留deteQt未使用的高阶特征向量；3) projeQt作用于广义模块度矩阵，基于量子态重叠构造Gram矩阵并做k-means聚类，无需递归即可一次提取多个社区。

Result: 在合成数据和小规模数据集上，递归二分的deteQt能恢复植入社区及冗余特征；在MNIST上，两种流程分别用于选取信息量大的像素与直接通过聚类分类图像，性能与标准社区检测器相当（competitive）。

Conclusion: 量子谱社区检测器可作为特征选择与聚类的可行流程组件，projeQt通过谱滤波与态重叠Gram矩阵聚类实现多社区一次性提取，具备实用竞争力。

Abstract: Community detection offers an alternative to conventional methods for data analysis tasks such as feature selection and clustering, and detecting communities with quantum algorithms is a promising avenue for quantum speedup. We investigate quantum spectral community detectors, such as the modularity-matrix algorithm deteQt, in pipelines for both tasks. We first generalise deteQt to multiple communities by recursive bisection, and show that it recovers planted communities, and hence redundant features, in synthetic and small datasets. We then propose projeQt, which applies a spectral filter to the encoded modularity matrix through the quantum singular value transformation, retaining higher-order eigenvectors that deteQt does not use. projeQt acts on the generalised modularity matrix and extracts several communities at once, without recursion, by k-means clustering of a Gram matrix built from quantum state overlaps. On MNIST we demonstrate both pipelines, selecting informative pixels and classifying images directly by clustering, and find them competitive with standard community detectors.

</details>


### [98] [Classical sampling of verifiable graph-state quantum advantage experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32757)
*Zhongpu Qiu,Yuqi Qing,Jielan Li,Hongxia Hao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 针对可验证量子优势提案在IBM硬件上的两个实例，作者用8块GPU经典采样，在质量与时间上均超越硬件，揭示了T门受误差检测约束只能置于电路末端这一结构性弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子优势演示需同时满足"经典难以复现"与"可验证"两个条件。近期提案通过制备带内建错误检测的图态实现可验证性，并通过非Clifford基测量或插入T门增加经典难度。作者质疑这类方案的经典硬度是否真实存在。

Method: 对IBM硬件上展示该提案的两个实例（70比特实例；64比特含314个T门实例）进行经典采样，使用8块GPU实现高效模拟，并将采样质量、保真度与运行时间同硬件结果对比；同时分析T门在图态错误检测约束下的可放置位置。

Result: 两个实例均可在8块GPU上以相当或更短时间完成采样，且质量高于硬件。70比特实例的样本来自理想输出分布；64比特含314个T门实例达到至少0.908的认证保真度，而硬件仅0.349。

Conclusion: 可验证性本身导致T门只能放在误差检测不可见的位置，从而被迫集中于电路末端，对经典计算成本贡献甚微。研究指出未来可验证优势实验必须控制的关键特征。

Abstract: Quantum computers are expected to outperform classical ones on some tasks, but a convincing demonstration must be both hard to reproduce classically and verifiable. A recent proposal achieves verifiability by preparing graph states with built-in error detection, so that the quality of the experiment can be certified without simulating it; hardness is added by measuring in a non-Clifford basis or by inserting T gates. We show that the two instances posted to demonstrate this proposal on IBM hardware can be sampled classically on eight graphics processing units, with higher quality than the hardware in comparable or shorter time. For the 70-qubit instance our samples are drawn from the ideal output distribution; for the 64-qubit instance with 314 T gates they come with a certified fidelity of at least 0.908, against 0.349 for the hardware. The reason lies in verification itself: T gates can only be placed where the error detection cannot see them, which pushes them towards the end of the circuit, where they add little classical cost. Our results identify the features that future verifiable advantage experiments must control.

</details>


### [99] [Programmable multipartite entanglement analogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32759)
*Long Chen,Qing Chun Yin,Zi Xuan Cai,Zhou He,Zhi Cai Yu,Zhihao Lan,Xiaohan Cui,Jian Wei You,Tie Jun Cui*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用时空超表面，在多自由度电磁场中实现"多体性"本身可编程，构建从二体到五体的类Bell与类GHZ不可分经典波态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠的经典类比（经典不可分波）有助于研究多体相干结构，但现有实现由生成架构决定，参与的自由度数目与身份基本固定，无法灵活调控多体性的组成与阶数。

Method: 采用时空超表面作为生成平台，在偏振、频率、轨道角动量、波矢方向与正交包络模式五个自由度上，独立且可重构地设定被相干链接的态空间身份与数目。

Result: 实验实现了完整的类Bell与类GHZ态家族，涵盖二体至五体阶数，并可在固定阶数下交换组成成分；所实现的五体态为经典电磁场中报道的最高多体阶数。

Conclusion: 将相干性在物理态空间间的分布确立为经典波的可编程属性，为在不涉及量子纠缠与非定域性的前提下研究多体量子态的特定结构提供了新平台。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement underpins the quantum information science. Its multipartite forms distribute coherence across several subsystems, but controlling their composition and order is increasingly difficult as more subsystems participate. Classical nonseparable waves provide accessible analogues of these structures, although existing implementations largely fix the participating degrees of freedom through their generating architectures. Here we overcome this constraint by making multipartiteness itself programmable in electromagnetic fields. A spatiotemporal metasurface reconfigures the identity and number of coherently linked state spaces independently across polarization, frequency, orbital angular momentum, wavevector direction, and orthogonal envelope modes. We realize complete Bell-like and Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger-like families from bipartite to five-partite order and exchange constituents at fixed order. To our knowledge, the five-partite state has the highest multipartite order experimentally realized in classical electromagnetic fields. These results establish the distribution of coherence across physical state spaces as a programmable property of classical waves and provide a platform for studying selected structures of multipartite quantum states without invoking quantum entanglement or nonlocality.

</details>


### [100] [Large-Scale Streaming Portfolio Optimization with Pauli Correlation Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32769)
*Vicente P. Soloviev,Josh Kirsopp*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Pauli Correlation Encoding（PCE）从单次历史快照的静态测试推进到滚动再平衡的流式部署，并与联合多割变体dPCE一起对比买入持有基线，验证其在千级资产规模下的实际部署价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大资产池的组合构建是组合选择问题，现有启发式方法在大规模下表现不佳；PCE用远少于“每资产一个量子比特”的量子比特数表示底层聚类问题，但此前仅在一个历史快照上验证过竞争力，缺乏贴近实盘滚动再平衡的评估。

Method: 构建流式部署框架：按滚动窗口重新估计相关结构与组合，提出两种流式变体——PCE本身与联合多割变体dPCE；在不同窗口长度、数千资产规模的组合、风险厌恶与交易成本设定、测量shot预算下，与Buy & Hold基线对比评估。

Result: 流式PCE与dPCE一致优于被动基线，包括在市场下行期两者均亏损但PCE亏损显著更小；PCE在规模化场景更实用，因dPCE的资源需求与对再平衡频率的敏感性都随组合规模增长更快。同时给出再平衡节奏、shot预算、交易成本感知等操作选择的重要性排序。

Conclusion: PCE的价值不局限于上涨市场，具备流式实盘部署潜力；大规模下应优先选择PCE而非dPCE，并应重点关注再平衡节奏与交易成本感知等关键操作参数。

Abstract: Classical portfolio construction over large asset universes is a combinatorial selection problem that existing heuristics do not solve well at scale. Pauli Correlation Encoding (PCE) addresses this by representing the underlying clustering problem with far fewer qubits than one per asset, and a prior study showed it is competitive with classical methods on a single historical snapshot. This paper moves beyond that static test to a streaming deployment, in which the correlation structure and the portfolio are both re-estimated on a rolling basis, as a live rebalancing system would actually operate. We introduce two streaming variants -- PCE itself and a joint multi-cut variant, dPCE -- and evaluate both against a Buy & Hold baseline across window lengths, portfolio sizes up to several thousand assets, risk-aversion and transaction-cost settings, and measurement-shot budgets. Streaming PCE and dPCE consistently outperform the passive baseline, including during a market downturn where both lose money but PCE loses markedly less -- evidence that the method's value is not confined to rising markets. PCE also proves the more practical choice at scale, since dPCE's resource requirements and its sensitivity to rebalancing frequency both grow faster with portfolio size. We further report which operational choices -- rebalancing cadence, shot budget, and transaction-cost awareness -- matter most for a real deployment, and which matter less than commonly assumed.

</details>


### [101] [Exploiting SU(2) Symmetry for Efficient Quantum State Preparation of Matrix Product States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32805)
*Etienne D'Argenlieu,Alessandro Summer,François Jamet,David P. Tew,Lachlan P. Lindoy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将DMRG得到的MPS以非阿贝尔SU(2)对称性编译为容错量子电路，Toffoli门数相比U(1)适配编译降低约3倍、相比稠密编译降低一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子算法（实时动力学、相位估计）依赖初始多体态的高效制备，而态制备是瓶颈；DMRG得到的MPS可顺序编译为容错电路，但如何进一步降低编译成本尚待研究。

Method: 利用自旋SU(2)非阿贝尔对称性：依据Wigner-Eckart定理，仅需对承载变分信息的约化张量做通用酉综合，磁结构由具有解析已知角度的Clebsch-Gordan变换恢复；在1D Hubbard、准1D Hubbard几何及Anderson杂质模型上以Toffoli计数为指标做容错资源估计。

Result: 在所考察体系中，Toffoli成本相对U(1)适配编译降低约3倍，相对标准稠密编译降低超过一个数量级。

Conclusion: 非阿贝尔对称性可显著降低将经典可处理多体基态传输到量子计算机的成本，有利于其在量子动力学与能量估计中的应用。

Abstract: Quantum computing offers a promising route to simulating strongly correlated systems, particularly for dynamical and spectral quantities whose classical computation can become prohibitively expensive. Fault-tolerant algorithms for these tasks, including real-time dynamics and phase estimation, assume efficient preparation of the initial many-body state, making state preparation an important bottleneck. Matrix product states obtained classically with the density matrix renormalization group can be compiled sequentially into fault-tolerant circuits. We exploit non-Abelian spin SU(2) symmetry in this compilation. By the Wigner-Eckart theorem, only the reduced tensors, which carry the variational information, need generic unitary synthesis. The magnetic structure is restored by Clebsch-Gordan transformations with analytically known angles. We benchmark the method on representative strongly correlated models, including the one-dimensional Hubbard model, quasi-one-dimensional Hubbard geometries, and Anderson impurity models, and perform fault-tolerant resource estimates using the Toffoli count as the primary cost metric. Across the systems considered, exploiting non-Abelian SU(2) symmetry reduces the Toffoli cost roughly threefold relative to U(1)-adapted compilation and by more than an order of magnitude relative to the standard dense compilation method. These results demonstrate how non-Abelian symmetries can substantially reduce the cost of transferring classically tractable many-body ground states to quantum computers, facilitating their use in quantum dynamics and energy estimation.

</details>


### [102] [Theory-Informed Quantum Error Mitigation for Shor Order Finding with Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32923)
*Stefano Markidis,Qingxin Yang,Erik Åsgrim,Ivy Peng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出理论驱动的量子误差缓解方法，用含学习增益的Fourier神经算子从含噪测量分布中恢复Shor阶估计结果，模拟测试精确恢复率从19%升至34%，IBM真机上正确阶估计数从35增至53。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子噪声扭曲电路测量结果的分布，但往往仍保留足以推断算法正确结果的信息；需要在Shor阶估计（order finding）中从含噪分布里恢复这一潜在信息。

Method: 理论驱动方法：首先建模电路错误如何使输出分布中的峰发生位移、展宽或减弱；以此模型指导合成训练数据生成，并设计带学习增益的Fourier神经算子（增益对保留的高阶Fourier模式进行衰减或放大）。对每个候选阶，用其理想分布与三次电路执行实测分布之间的相关性求平均得分；网络调整这些得分以引导阶估计，并确定各理想分布归一化混合的权重。

Result: 1,351个一般模幂（modular exponentiation）模拟测试（按电路配置留出）：精确阶恢复率由19%提升至34%，与理想分布的中位Jensen-Shannon散度由0.64降至0.32（下降50%）。5台IBM量子处理器上执行的101个Mersenne旋转电路：在模拟中训练并校准的模型将正确阶估计数由35提升至53。

Conclusion: 即使噪声使算法结构几乎不可见，算法结构仍可被恢复；理论建模与神经算子结合可有效从含噪分布中提取有价值信息。

Abstract: Quantum noise distorts the distribution of circuit measurement outcomes, often leaving enough information to infer the algorithm's correct result. To recover this latent information from the noisy distribution in Shor order finding, we introduce a theory-informed quantum error mitigation method. We model how circuit errors shift, broaden, or weaken peaks in the output distribution. These models guide both the generation of synthetic training data and the design of a Fourier neural operator with a learned gain that attenuates or amplifies higher retained Fourier modes. We score each candidate order by averaging the correlations between its ideal distribution and the distributions measured in three circuit executions. The network adjusts these scores to guide the order estimate and determine the weights of a normalized mixture of the ideal distributions. On 1,351 simulated test cases for general modular exponentiation, held out by circuit configuration, exact order recovery rises from 19% to 34%, and the median Jensen-Shannon divergence from the ideal distribution falls by 50%, from 0.64 to 0.32. On 101 Mersenne rotation circuits executed across five IBM quantum processors, a separate model trained and calibrated in simulation increases the number of correct order estimates from 35 to 53. Algorithmic structure can remain recoverable even when noise makes it barely visible.

</details>


### [103] [Quantum kernels on breast data: concentration, surrogates, and two ways an advantage gets manufactured](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32942)
*Rana Saad,Achraf Toufah,Moulay Youssef El Hafidi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究以三个基准实验（全部为8量子比特保真度核的精确经典态矢量模拟，推理路径中不含量子成分）系统检验了"量子核优势"声明的稳健性。所有三个有效实验组均为负结果：所谓优势在完整比较方法集与选择历史下消失，比较方法选择与种子选择各自产生的效应均大于任何测得的量子-经典差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子核在乳腺数据上的"优势"通常被简化为单一数字：单一量子方案、单一对照方法、单一测试划分。作者要追问的是：当完整对照方法集与选择历史（模型选择、种子选择、数据预处理、带宽、核族）全部可见时，这个数字究竟测量了什么。核心动机是指出该数字在很大程度上是选择效应的产物，而非量子-经典的真实差距。

Method: 三个有效基准：（1）WDBC（威斯康星乳腺癌诊断）上10个训练集规模的样本效率扫描，模型选择仅限训练数据；（2）1,200例M-SYNTH模拟乳腺钼靶的原始 vs 处理后对照，配以匹配的闭式去量化代理，同输入下比较五种经典方法与十一种核族；（3）BreastMNIST上30个随机种子的选择膨胀测量。另有CMMD D1临床组因冻结划分中患者序列号泄露标签（另文报告）而被撤销结果资格，仅作示例。全部为8量子比特保真度核的精确经典态矢量模拟，推理路径无量子成分。

Result: 三个有效实验组均为负结果。M-SYNTH原始数据上，相对调优RBF-SVM的表观优势+0.1736（p=5.2e-06），在相同输入下改为随机森林后降至+0.0019（p=0.937），且验证集上Laplacian核胜出。WDBC上量子核在每一个训练集规模下均低于所选经典方案，200个配对子样本中仅2个领先。BreastMNIST上30种子取最优带来+0.0210的选择膨胀。唯一例外：带宽2π时匹配的闭式高斯代理未能复现M-SYNTH原始核（0.5410对0.7045，p=6.3e-06）。

Conclusion: 比较方法的选择与选择历史各自产生的效应，均大于所有测得的量子-经典差异；因此单数字式"量子核优势"声明缺乏信息量。作者提出一套报告标准，同时消除比较方法选择偏差与种子/模型选择膨胀。

Abstract: Quantum-kernel advantage claims on breast data are usually a single number: one quantum arm, one comparator, one test split. We asked what that number measures once the full comparator set and selection history are visible. We report three active benchmarks, all exact classical statevector simulations of 8-qubit fidelity kernels with no quantum component in the inference path: a sample-efficiency sweep on Wisconsin Diagnostic Breast Cancer at ten training-set sizes; raw-versus-processed arms on 1,200 M-SYNTH simulated mammograms with a matched closed-form dequantisation surrogate, five classical methods at the same input and eleven kernel families; and a 30-seed selection-inflation measurement on BreastMNIST. A CMMD D1 clinical arm is withdrawn as a result arm because its frozen splits leak the label through patient serial order, a defect reported in a separate study; it is retained only as a worked example. All three active arms are negative. On M-SYNTH raw an apparent +0.1736 (p = 5.2e-06) against a tuned RBF-SVM falls to +0.0019 (p = 0.937) against a random forest at the same input, and a Laplacian kernel wins on validation. On WDBC, with model selection confined to training data, the quantum kernel is below the selected classical arm at every training-set size, with 2 of 200 paired subsamples ahead. Best-of-30 seed selection adds +0.0210 on BreastMNIST. One result does not fit: at bandwidth 2pi the matched closed-form Gaussian surrogate failed to reproduce the M-SYNTH raw kernel (0.5410 against 0.7045, p = 6.3e-06). Comparator choice and selection each produce effects larger than any quantum-classical difference we measured; we give a reporting standard that removes both.

</details>


### [104] [Iterative Petz Protocols for Quantum State Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32995)
*Lea Lautenbacher,Felix Ahnefeld,Susana F. Huelga,Martin B. Plenio*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出迭代Petz恢复（IPR）协议：从无偏参考态出发，用每次恢复输出构造下一轮Petz映射的自适应多拷贝策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声无处不在，近似逆转噪声是量子技术核心挑战；Petz映射虽为物理可容许的恢复信道，但性能强烈依赖参考态选择。

Method: 引入迭代Petz恢复协议（自适应多拷贝，无偏参考态起步、逐轮更新参考态）；证明协议稳定性；构建层析辅助的有限拷贝实现并显式控制误差传播与保真度损失；对量子比特退极化噪声推导每个纯输入保真度的精确递推，对单轴Pauli噪声推导六态Pauli平均的精确递推并与Haar平均数值比较；将有限拷贝IPR与基于局域/集体测量的测量-制备策略进行基准对比。

Result: 建立稳定性；给出退极化噪声下纯输入保真度精确递推与单轴Pauli噪声六态Pauli平均精确递推；数值揭示有限资源下IPR与测量-制备策略的权衡差异。

Conclusion: IPR通过自适应更新参考态缓解Petz映射对参考态的敏感性，且可在有限拷贝下可控实现，为噪声逆转提供了可基准化的实用方案。

Abstract: Noise is omnipresent in quantum systems, and thus, approximately reversing this noise is a central challenge towards quantum technologies. The Petz map provides a physically admissible recovery channel, but its performance depends critically on the choice of reference state. Here, we introduce and investigate the iterative Petz recovery (IPR) protocol, an adaptive multicopy strategy that begins from an unbiased reference state and uses each recovered output to construct the Petz map for the next iteration. We establish the stability of the protocol and develop a tomography-assisted finite-copy implementation with explicit control of error propagation and fidelity loss. For qubit depolarizing noise, we derive an exact recurrence for the fidelity of every pure input. For single-axis Pauli noise, we derive an exact recurrence for the six-state Pauli average and compare it numerically with the Haar average. We further benchmark finite-copy IPR against measure-and-prepare strategies based on local and collective measurements, revealing the associated finite-resource tradeoffs.

</details>


### [105] [Polynomial Master Equations of Open Quantum Systems and an Analysis on the Critical Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33027)
*Miku Ishizaki,Jaeha Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种系统性方法，将开放量子系统的非马尔可夫动力学表述为与高阶矩阵多项式相关联的线性微分方程（而非传统的一阶积分-微分方程），从而支持一种"局部确定性"解释，并通过两量子比特模型和无界哈密顿量的无限维系统两个精确可解例子加以演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，非马尔可夫性被视为源于系统演化的全局历史，通常用一阶积分-微分方程描述，难以用一阶微分方程刻画。作者希望考察一种替代性的"局部确定性"解释（见 arXiv:2607.09651），即系统的演化仅由其局部行为决定，因此需要发展出与之匹配的高阶线性微分方程框架。

Method: 建立系统性的推导方法，得到与高阶矩阵多项式对应的非马尔可夫线性微分方程；并以两个可精确分析的实例进行验证：（1）基于最简单的非平凡两量子比特模型；（2）涉及定义在无限维量子系统上的无界哈密顿量。此外，分析系统动力学发生定性变化的临界参数。

Result: 在两个精确可解模型中成功构造出相应的高阶多项式微分方程；识别出动力学发生定性变化的临界参数，并说明所导出的多项式方程如何刻画这些现象。

Conclusion: 开放量子系统的非马尔可夫动力学可以在局部确定性的框架下，用高阶矩阵多项式线性微分方程一致地描述；该方法对有限维与无限维系统均适用，并能自然地解释临界参数处的动力学转变。

Abstract: The dynamics of open quantum systems exhibits in general a non-Markovian behavior, lying typically beyond the description of first-order differential equations. Traditional methods commonly turned to first-order integro-differential equations, which allowed for the standard interpretation of non-Markovianity as having its origin in the global history of the system evolution. We examine the possibility of an alternative, locally deterministic interpretation [arXiv:2607.09651] of the working of its dynamical hysteresis, where the evolution of a system is determined by its local behavior alone. The present work elaborates on our systematic method in deriving non-Markovian linear differential equations associated with matrix polynomials of higher order. Our methods will be demonstrated with exact analyses on two illustrative examples: one built upon the simplest non-trivial model concerning a two-qubit system, and another involving an unbounded Hamiltonian defined on an infinite-dimensional quantum system. We also analyze the critical parameters at which qualitative changes are observed in the system dynamics, and reveal how our polynomial equations account for the phenomena.

</details>


### [106] [Class-resolved sifting for quantum key distribution with photon pairs hyperentangled in polarization and transverse spatial variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33055)
*Murilo H. Magiotto,Millena B. Novaes,Wesley B. Cardoso,Ardiley T. Avelar,Guilherme L. Zanin,Rafael M. Gomes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"分类筛选"方法，将偏振—横向空间超纠缠光子对的每一个探测事件按所测自由度/基归入类别，分别估计错误率与密钥率，从而恢复独立筛选的密钥产额，并把类别间比较转化为两个自由度交叉依赖性的定量一致性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超纠缠源每个光子对可承载两个密钥比特，但不同测量组合对应的筛选效率、错误率差异未被区分，导致密钥产额估计不准确、且缺乏对偏振与空间自由度之间交叉依赖的可检验判据。

Method: 对双晶体SPDC源在中分傅里叶平面读出数据做分类筛选：按"哪些自由度在相容基下被测量"划分类别，逐类评估误码率与密钥率；并按偏振分析器设置分辨数据；此外将超纠缠协议与单变量4维系统（两个无偏基）作对比分析。

Result: 分类筛选复现了独立筛选的密钥产额；测得的两自由度依赖性低于该源预期的空间—偏振耦合，处于当前统计灵敏度边缘；按偏振设置分辨后发现某一读出臂中空间误差对偏振设置约有2倍的依赖，归因于测量装置而非光源；单变量4维编码仅在四结果读出把符号误差控制在百分之几以内时才优于超纠缠方案。

Conclusion: 分类筛选是提升超纠缠QKD密钥产额估计可靠性的有效手段，并可作为自由度间交叉依赖的一致性检验工具；超纠缠的优势在于基的张量积可分解、实现简单且错误可按自由度归因，而单变量高维编码的优势在于可接入非积基、噪声容忍度更高；装置引起的基依赖效应与旁信道分析直接相关。

Abstract: Photon pairs hyperentangled in polarization and transverse spatial variables can carry two key bits per pair in entanglement-based quantum key distribution. We develop class-resolved sifting for such sources: every detected pair is assigned to a class according to which degrees of freedom were measured in compatible bases, and error and key rates are estimated separately in each class. Applied to previously reported data from a two-crystal spontaneous parametric down-conversion source read out in intermediate fractional-Fourier planes, the method recovers the key yield of independent sifting and turns the comparison between classes into a quantitative consistency test for cross-dependencies between the two degrees of freedom. The measured dependence lies below the spatial-polarization coupling predicted for the source, at the edge of the present statistical sensitivity. Resolving the data by polarization-analyzer setting, we find an approximately twofold dependence of the spatial error on the polarization setting in one readout arm, attributable to the measurement apparatus rather than to the source and relevant to basis-dependent side channels. We compare the hyperentangled protocol with a single 4-dimensional system measured in two mutually unbiased bases. Fundamentally, both yield the same key per detected pair and obey the same entropic bound, but they differ in their tensor-product structure: hyperentanglement factors the bases into separate two-outcome measurements, which are simpler to implement and allow errors to be attributed to each degree of freedom, whereas a single variable gives access to non-product bases that can increase the noise tolerance. For the present source, whose polarization coherence is limited by partial which-crystal erasure, a single-variable spatial encoding would yield more key only if its four-outcome readout kept the symbol error within a few percent.

</details>


### [107] [Finite-Temperature Superradiant Criticality and Thermal Emission in the Open Dicke Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33063)
*Gabriele Orlando,Samuel Napoli,Salvatore Savasta,Omar Di Stefano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了开放Dicke模型在有限温度下、不限制系统-热库耦合强度的超辐射相变理论：腔模与原子系综分别耦合零温与温度T的独立热库，发现在广泛谱密度下热库不移动温度依赖的临界耦合，极化激元在临界耦合附近有限区间内过阻尼；升温使相变移至更强耦合，超辐射相中光-物质解耦更快；该框架给出任意参数下的反射与热发射谱，并识别出系统在特定频率同时表现为完美吸收体与完美发射体的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dicke模型超辐射相变附近最低频激发软化，其损耗不再能作为弱微扰处理，现有弱耦合开放系统理论失效，因此需要发展无系统-热库耦合强度限制的有限温度相变理论。

Method: 构建开放系统有限温度理论：腔模耦合零温热库、原子系综耦合温度T热库，假设自旋与热库保持热化；不限制系统-热库耦合强度，对广泛谱密度求解，计算温度依赖临界耦合、极化激元阻尼行为以及反射谱与热发射谱。

Result: 对广泛谱密度，热库不移动温度依赖的临界耦合；极化激元在临界耦合附近有限区间内过阻尼；升温使相变移至更强耦合；超辐射相中光与物质解耦更快；框架给出正常相与超辐射相任意参数下的反射与热发射谱；并识别出系统在特定频率同时为完美吸收体与完美发射体的条件。

Conclusion: 热库在广泛谱密度下不改变温度依赖的临界耦合，但显著影响临界区极化激元的过阻尼行为；该无耦合强度限制的有限温度理论可定量描述正常相与超辐射相的光谱响应，为基态凝聚相关实验提供可比对的理论基础。

Abstract: Near the superradiant phase transition of the Dicke model, the lowest-frequency excitation softens and its losses can no longer be treated as a weak perturbation. We develop a finite-temperature theory of this transition in the open system, with no restriction on the strength of the system-bath coupling. The cavity mode and the atomic ensemble are coupled to independent reservoirs, the first at zero temperature and the second at temperature $T$, and we assume that the spins remain thermalized with their reservoir. We find that the reservoirs do not shift the temperature-dependent critical coupling for a broad class of spectral densities, while the polariton becomes overdamped in a finite interval of couplings around it. As expected, raising the temperature moves the transition to stronger couplings, and in the superradiant phase we find a faster decoupling of light and matter. The theoretical framework developed here yields the reflection and thermal emission spectra for arbitrary parameters in both the normal and the superradiant phase, and provides a basis for comparison with experiments on systems displaying ground-state condensation. We also identify conditions under which the system behaves, at specific frequencies, as both a perfect absorber and a perfect emitter.

</details>


### [108] [Robust growth of $α$-tantalum on sapphire at room temperature using atomically thin titanium seed layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33112)
*Anand Kamlapure,Lucas Hak,Kishore Kumar Thiyagarajan,Hozanna Miro,Marc Beekman,Matvey Finkel,Leonardo DiCarlo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出以超薄Ti籽晶层在室温下于蓝宝石上生长α相Ta薄膜的稳健路线，显著拓宽沉积窗口，CPW谐振器测得TLS限制品质因数达到同类Ta/蓝宝石器件先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ta基超导电路因低微波损耗受关注，但高质量α相Ta薄膜的可重复生长困难，薄膜性质对生长条件高度敏感，急需拓宽工艺窗口的可靠方法。

Method: 对比三种路线：Ti籽晶层+室温生长、无籽晶高温生长、Nb籽晶+室温生长；通过结构与表面表征，并用量子区测量的共面波导（CPW）谐振器定量微波损耗。

Result: Ti籽晶路线获得与同类几何Ta/蓝宝石CPW谐振器先进水平相当的TLS限制品质因数；变温测量显示高温生长薄膜及经延长BOE处理的薄膜出现额外的温度激活损耗。

Conclusion: 超薄籽晶层工程是实现可重复、低损耗α-Ta薄膜的可靠替代路线，并提示需进一步研究主导Ta相形成的界面机制。

Abstract: Tantalum (Ta) based superconducting circuits have attracted attention for their low microwave loss. However, reproducible growth of high-quality $α$-phase Ta films remains challenging because film properties are highly sensitive to growth conditions. We present a robust, room-temperature route for $α$-Ta growth on sapphire substrates using an ultrathin Ti seed layer, which significantly widens the accessible deposition parameter space. We compare this approach to Ta films grown at high temperature without a seed layer and at room temperature with a Nb seed. We assess films using structural and surface characterization and quantify microwave loss with coplanar-waveguide (CPW) resonators measured in the quantum regime. We obtain two-level-system (TLS)-limited quality factors comparable to the state of the art for Ta-on-sapphire CPW resonators with similar geometries. In addition, temperature-dependent measurements reveal that high-temperature-grown films and films subjected to extended buffered-oxide etch treatment exhibit additional temperature activated loss, highlighting the role of growth route and post-processing in microwave performance. Our results establish ultrathin seed-layer engineering as a reliable alternative route to reproducible, low-loss $α$-Ta films and motivate closer study of the interfacial mechanisms that govern Ta phase formation.

</details>


### [109] [Classical algorithms for sparse random Hamiltonians via free probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33128)
*Matthew Ding,Robbie King,Thomas Schuster*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对稀疏随机非局域哈密顿量，设计了运行时间为 $(1/\varepsilon)^{2+o(1)}$ 的经典算法，可精确计算能谱涨落、热力学量以及时序与乱序关联函数，甚至覆盖晚期演化、低温与临界点等区域，从而削弱了此类模型中"量子优势"的理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子优势在平均情形问题上的存在性是物理与计算机科学的重大开放问题，其中量子多体系统模拟被认为是最广泛的候选场景。然而，符号问题与纠缠增长导致经典方法失效，而支持或反对此类量子优势的理论证据仍然稀缺，因此作者希望在稀疏随机非局域哈密顿量这一具体模型中给出定量判据。

Method: 不直接模拟量子态或算符，而是先计算模型的平均行为，再叠加一层简短的、依赖具体实例的修正项；利用自由概率论（free probability theory），由短时数据外推长时行为。

Result: 对任意亚指数精度 $\varepsilon$，算法运行时间为 $(1/\varepsilon)^{2+o(1)}$，与量子模拟几乎相同；该复杂度在晚期演化时间、低温以及临界点附近依然成立，而这些区域现有经典方法需要指数时间。算法可精确计算能谱涨落、热力学期望值、时序关联函数与乱序关联函数（OTOC）。

Conclusion: 在稀疏随机非局域哈密顿量上，经典算法可在与量子模拟相当的复杂度下完成多项任务，说明该模型并非可靠的量子优势候选；同时也提示"平均行为 + 有限阶实例修正 + 自由概率外推"可作为分析随机多体模型的通用框架。

Abstract: The search for quantum advantage in average-case problems is a pressing open question in physics and computer science. The widest conjectured example of such an advantage is in simulation of quantum many-body systems, where sign problems and entanglement growth cause the breakdown of many classical methods. Nonetheless, theoretical evidence for or against such an advantage remains scarce. Here, we investigate this in sparse random non-local Hamiltonians. We design a classical algorithm for such Hamiltonians that enables accurate computation of spectral fluctuations, thermal values, and time-ordered and out-of-time-order correlation functions. For any subexponential precision $\varepsilon$, our algorithm runs in time $(1/\varepsilon)^{2+o(1)}$, nearly identical to a quantum simulation. Crucially, this applies even at late evolution times, low temperatures, and near critical points, where existing classical methods require exponential time. Rather than directly simulating a quantum state or operator, our algorithm first computes the model's mean behavior and then a short hierarchy of instance-dependent corrections. These corrections predict long-time behavior based on short-time data using free probability theory.

</details>


### [110] [A Koashi--Imoto Criterion for Holevo-Rate Visible Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33129)
*Anthony M. Polloreno*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文刻画了"编码器已知经典标签序列"的有限无记忆量子源在共享经典随机性、无共享纠缠条件下Holevo速率可达的充要条件：在信号集的极大Koashi–Imoto分解中，每个信号在其所占每个扇区的量子部分上必须是纯态；对量子比特即信号全为纯态或两两对易。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典标签（visible）编码下，Holevo信息是渐近最优速率，但实际可用的资源是共享经典随机性而非共享纠缠。需要确定在缺少纠缠时，何时仍能达到Holevo速率，以及何时会出现不可消除的速率间隙。

Method: 以信号集的极大Koashi–Imoto分解为工具，将各扇区的量子部分与经典标签分离；对量子比特情形化为纯态或成对对易的判据。逆定理（converse）方面：假设存在一列可见码在局部误差趋于零的同时速率逼近Holevo信息，对其施加Fawzi–Renner恢复映射，再结合Baghali Khanian与Winter关于盲压缩的解耦（decoupling）界导出矛盾。

Result: 得到可达性的精确充要条件：每个信号在其所占每个扇区的量子部分上为纯态（量子比特：全纯或两两对易）。反之，若任一被占据的量子部分为混态，则任何平均局部误差足够小的码，其速率都一致地（关于分组长度一致）与Holevo信息保持正间隙。

Conclusion: 无共享纠缠时Holevo速率的可达性由Koashi–Imoto分解中信号量子部分的纯性完全决定；混态部分导致速率–误差权衡中出现不可消除的间隙，恢复映射与盲压缩解耦界是该逆定理的核心技术。

Abstract: For a finite memoryless quantum source whose encoder knows the sequence of classical labels, we determine when the Holevo rate is achievable with shared classical randomness and no shared entanglement. In the maximal Koashi--Imoto decomposition of the signal ensemble, this occurs exactly when each signal is pure on the quantum part of every sector it occupies. For qubits, this means that the signals are all pure or commute pairwise. If any occupied quantum part is mixed, every code with sufficiently small mean local error has rate bounded away from the Holevo information, uniformly in blocklength. To prove the converse, we apply Fawzi--Renner recovery to a hypothetical sequence of visible codes approaching the Holevo rate at vanishing local error, then invoke a decoupling bound of Baghali Khanian and Winter for blind compression.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [Localization in one-dimensional magnetic quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31384)
*Andrew Osborne,Rhine Samajdar,Andrew A. Houck*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究图上准周期外场下的一维晶格中，图拓扑如何调控金属-绝缘体转变，给出临界局域化、指数局域化、平带与有限支撑态共存及可解迁移率边的模型，并用玻色子精确对角化表明在位相互作用驱动局域态趋向扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Aubry-André-Harper 模型中金属-绝缘体转变源于最近邻跃迁与准周期在位势的能量竞争；作者希望引入图拓扑（磁通引起的干涉）作为额外的竞争机制，考察其如何改变局域化性质。

Method: 在一维晶格上施加准周期外场，但底层图为非平庸拓扑结构（含磁通/干涉回路），通过构造性模型分析单粒子本征态；并采用玻色子动力学的精确对角化计算研究相互作用效应。

Result: (1) 构造出所有色散本征态均为临界局域化的例子；(2) 也存在色散态指数局域化的模型；(3) 上述现象可与大量简并的有限支撑本征态（平带）共存；(4) 构造出迁移率边仅由图结构导致、且可精确求解的模型；(5) 相互作用下局域态向扩展态演化，行为类似常规 AAH 情形。

Conclusion: 图结构（磁干涉）与在位相互作用共同约束准周期晶格中的输运，为设计可调控局域化与迁移率边的平台提供了统一框架。

Abstract: The celebrated Aubry-André-Harper model undergoes a metal-insulator transition akin to Anderson localization due to an energetic competition between nearest-neighbor hopping and a quasiperiodic on-site potential.
